(two `card-in-play` conditions + `site-protected` + `playable-at-tapped-site`; influence # 12 encodes "greater than 11").
- Faction `playableAt` `when` context — Dragon's-lair and Under-deeps exclusions. `siteMatchesEntry` (`legal-actions/site.ts`) now exposes two extra paths on the site context: `site.lairOf` (the resident Dragon's card id, absent for ordinary sites — a faction excludes Dragon's lairs via `{ "site.lairOf": { "$exists": false } }`) and `site.isUnderDeepsSurface` (true when the site is the roll-0 surface entrance of some Under-deeps site — computed by `isUnderDeepsSurfaceSite` in `legal-actions/organization-companies.ts` and passed in by every faction/ally caller). Together with the `under-deeps` keyword these express "not an Under-deeps site or surface site thereof". The faction-influence-check resolver context's `bearer` also now carries `bearer.keywords` (the influencing character's subgrouping keywords, e.g. `leader`), so a faction's printed Modifications can target the influencer by keyword. Used by A Few Recruits (ba-80): "Balrog specific. Playable at a tapped or untapped non-Dragon's lair: Dark-hold, Shadow-hold, or Ruins & Lairs — the site cannot be an Under-deeps site or surface site thereof — if the influence check is greater than 8. Modifications: The Balrog (+3), leader (+2)." — a `playableAt` `{ any: true, when }` entry (`site.siteType $in [dark-hold, shadow-hold, ruins-and-lairs]` + `$not keywords $includes under-deeps` + `site.isUnderDeepsSurface $ne true` + `site.lairOf $exists false`), a `play-flag playable-at-tapped-site`, and two `check-modifier influence` effects (+3 gated `bearer.name: "The Balrog"`, +2 gated `bearer.keywords $includes leader`). Influence # 9 encodes "greater than 8".
- `play-condition` `requires: 'active-company'` on **Stage permanent-events** — a Stage-alignment permanent-event carrying an `active-company` play-condition declares its own site-phase timing (an explicit exception to rule 5.F1, which otherwise confines Stage resource permanent-events to the organization phase). The site-phase play path (`legal-actions/site.ts` `playResourcesActions`) lets such a card through the 5.F1 skip and evaluates the `active-company` condition against the active company's context (`buildActiveCompanyContext`, exported from `legal-actions/organization.ts`: `{ site: { name, type }, company: { characterNames, itemNames, allyNames } }`); the organization-phase path (`legal-actions/organization-events.ts`) skips **any** permanent-event with an `active-company` play-condition so it is never offered there. A bare (target-less) match enters `cardsInPlay` and scores its printed MP normally (Stage cards are exempt from the MEWH §4 flat-1 clamp). Used by Delver's Harvest (wh-65): "Playable during the site phase if one of your companies enters the Deep Mines site." — 1 miscellaneous MP, `{ "site.name": "Deep Mines" }`.
- `cancel-card-effects` — generic "cancels the effects of X" primitive. While the carrying card is in play, any active constraint whose **source card** is named in `cardNames` is suppressed (its effect treated as absent), matched by source card name so an unrelated card sharing the same constraint kind is untouched. Checked at the top of `applyOneConstraint` (`legal-actions/pending.ts` `constraintSuppressedByCancelEffect`). Also new: `force-return-to-origin` now exposes `underDeepsMove` in its condition context (true when the moving company's origin or destination carries the `under-deeps` keyword — `findForcingEnvironment` in `mh-hazard-play.ts`), and the `only-creatures-keyed-to-site` constraint kind (Secret Passage tw-325 via `on-event: self-enters-play` → `add-constraint`) drops any hazard-creature play against the target company that is not keyed to its destination site by site-type/site-name (`applyOnlyCreaturesKeyedToSite`). Used by The Way is Shut (dm-98): "A company moving to or from an Under-deeps site must return to its site of origin. Additionally, cancels the effects of Secret Passage and Secret Entrance. Cannot be duplicated." — `force-return-to-origin` (`{ underDeepsMove: true }`), `cancel-card-effects` (["Secret Passage", "Secret Entrance"], suppressing Secret Entrance's `no-creatures-keyed-to-site` and Secret Passage's `only-creatures-keyed-to-site`), and `duplication-limit` scope game. The `cancel-return-and-site-tap` `add-constraint` kind (scope `"turn"`, target a company) is now genuinely consulted rather than being an inert marker: `findForcingEnvironment` (`mh-hazard-play.ts`, rule 5.31) short-circuits to "no forcing environment" for a company carrying the constraint, and `applyTapSitesInPlayOnResolve` (`chain-reducer.ts`, `tap-sites-in-play`) skips tapping that company's current site, both checked via `constraintsOnCompany` (`pending.ts`). Installed either from a grant-action apply (Promptings of Wisdom wh-34 / Piercing All Shadows wh-47: a ranger taps during organization to protect his own company) or directly from a short-event's `on-event: self-enters-play` → `add-constraint` (new switch case in `reducer-events.ts`, company resolved from `action.targetCharacterId`). Used by Govern the Storms (wh-45): "Cancel all hazard effects for the rest of the turn that: force a sorcery-using character's company to return to its site of origin or would tap its current or new site. Unless he is a Ringwraith, the sorcery using character makes a corruption check modified by -4." — a `play-target` character filter (`$or` over `target.race: "ringwraith"` and `target.skills $includes "sorcery"`, the same "any Ringwraith qualifies" convention as Deeper Shadow le-179's shadow-magic filter), the `add-constraint cancel-return-and-site-tap` effect, and `enqueue-corruption-check` (`modifier: -4`, `when $not target.race: "ringwraith"`).
- `play-condition` `requires: 'company-context'` on character-targeting permanent-events — a generic DSL `condition` evaluated against the **play-target character's company** (per target company, in both the organization and site phase char-target play paths), exposing `{ site: { name, type, isOwnWizardhaven }, company: { characterNames, itemNames, allyNames, playedUniqueHeroFactionAtFreeHold } }`. `itemNames` aggregates every item / attached permanent event borne by any company character, so a card can gate on "in the same company as `<named card>`". `site.isOwnWizardhaven` is `true` only when the company's current site is one of the **player's own Wizardhavens** (a Fallen-wizard haven, or a Hidden-Haven-converted site, via `isHavenForPlayer`) — the meaning of "at one of your Wizardhavens [{H}]", distinct from a generic METW Haven / MELE Darkhaven that merely shares `type: "haven"`. Used by Squire of the Hunt (wh-95): "Playable on one of your warrior characters at one of your Wizardhavens [{H}]" via `{ "site.isOwnWizardhaven": true }` — a stage permanent-event that also grants +1 prowess/+1 body/+1 direct influence (three `stat-modifier` effects, the two combat bonuses from the printed body/prowess icons), carries `control-restriction` cost 2 sources general/fallen-wizard, is gated `player-state` on `player.avatar: "Alatar"`, and filters the target to a non-avatar `warrior`; the same shape fits Gandalf's Friend wh-98 / Pallando's Apprentice wh-104. `playedUniqueHeroFactionAtFreeHold` is `true` only during the site phase and only once the active company has, this site phase, successfully played a unique hero faction at a Free-hold ≠ Bag End (tracked on `SitePhaseState.uniqueHeroFactionPlayedAtFreeHold`, set in `resolveInfluenceAttemptRoll`). Evaluated via `matchesCompanyContextCondition` (`reducer-utils.ts`) in `legal-actions/organization-events.ts` and `legal-actions/site.ts`. Used by To Fealty Sworn (ba-33): "Playable on a Hobbit: in the same company as Return of the King or during the same site phase his company plays a unique hero faction at a Free-hold [{F}] (not Bag End). The Hobbit gains the warrior skill, +2 prowess, and +5 direct influence against the Hobbits faction and characters with Bag End as a home site." — the two conditional +5 DI bonuses ride `stat-modifier` (`direct-influence`) with `when` gates keyed on `reason` (`influence-check` → `target.homesite` includes Bag End; `faction-influence-check` → `faction.name` is Hobbits), the warrior grant is `grant-skill`, and "not duplicated on a given Hobbit" is `duplication-limit` scope `character`.
- `play-target` `target: 'item'` — item-attached permanent events. A resource permanent event played "with"/"on" an item borne by one of the active player's own characters (site-phase only): the item legal-action emitter (`legal-actions/site.ts`) offers one `play-permanent-event` per company-character item whose definition matches the `filter` (context `{ target: { name, keywords, subtype } }`), carrying the chosen item on `targetItemInstanceId`. On resolution the card enters its controller's `cardsInPlay` bound via the new `CardInPlay.attachedToItem` (chain-reducer `resolvePermanentEvent`), and its `stat-modifier` effects flow to the item's bearer — `collectCharacterEffects` (`effects/resolver.ts`) now also collects effects from any `cardsInPlay` entry whose `attachedToItem` matches one of the character's active items, so the bonus rides the normal cap/override/combat pipeline (including `computeCombatProwess`'s `enemy.race` gating). The card is discarded by the `discardOrphanedItemAttachedEvents` postReduce sweep (`reducer-utils.ts`) once no character bears the host item. Companions: `play-condition` `requires: 'site-type'` gates the site; `duplication-limit` `scope: 'item'` (`countItemAttachedCopies`) limits copies per item; the new `play-flag: 'tap-bearer-on-play'` taps the item's bearer as the play cost (bearer must be untapped, enforced in the emitter). Used by Barrow-blade (dm-119): "Tap the bearer of a Dagger of Westernesse during the site phase at a Ruins & Lairs [{R}] and play this with the Dagger. Dagger receives +1 prowess (+3 versus Undead and Nazgûl). Cannot be duplicated on a given Dagger." — modeled as +1 prowess always plus +2 prowess `when: { reason: 'combat', enemy.race: { $in: ['undead', 'nazgul'] } }` (net +3 versus those). The same shape fits Free to Choose (dm-133), "Playable on an item that normally gives 3 corruption points or more."

- `play-condition` `requires: 'target-company'` alignment gate on hazard creatures — the creature-keying path (`legal-actions/movement-hazard.ts`) evaluates a `target-company` play-condition against the defending company via `buildTargetCompanyConditionContext(state, resourcePlayer, targetCompany, defenderAlignmentLabel(resourcePlayer.alignment))`, exposing `{ company: { alignment, characterNames, homeSites, maxUntappedWarriorProwess, covert } }`. `defenderAlignmentLabel` maps a Wizard player's companies to `"hero"` and every other alignment to its own label (`"ringwraith"`/`"fallen-wizard"`/`"balrog"`) — distinct from `detainmentAlignmentLabel`, which additionally folds Fallen-wizard into `"hero"` only for detainment determination. So `{ "company.alignment": "hero" }` restricts a creature to Wizard companies and `{ "company.alignment": { "$ne": "hero" } }` restricts to non-hero (minion) companies. `company.covert` is `isCovertCompany(company, owner, state)` computed inline (`true` = covert, `false` = overt), letting a condition combine alignment with covert/overt status. Used by Olog Warlords (ba-12): "Trolls. Three strikes (playable only against hero companies)." — one `play-condition` `target-company` with `{ "company.alignment": "hero" }` (the exact inverse of Sons of Kings le-91, which uses `$ne: "hero"` for "playable only against minion companies"). Also used by Elves upon Errantry (le-70): "Elves. Four strikes (playable only against minion companies)." — same `$ne: "hero"` gate as le-91, keyed `{w}{w}{b}{f}` (wilderness×2/border/free, single merged `regionTypes` entry per the `regionTypesMatch` per-type-count OR semantics). Also used by Landroval (le-81): "Two strikes (playable only against an overt minion company)" — `{ "company.alignment": { "$ne": "hero" }, "company.covert": false }` (plain-object implicit AND), the first card to combine the alignment gate with `company.covert`, plus the printed `combat-attacker-chooses-defenders` rule and `{w}{w}{b}{s}` keying (wilderness×2/border/shadow, single merged `regionTypes` entry).

- `seized-by-terror-check` + `play-discard-cost` — a hazard short-event played on a **character** in the active M/H company enqueues a `seized-by-terror-roll` on chain resolution (`chain-reducer.ts`): the character's player rolls 2d6 + mind, and on `roll + mind < threshold` the character **splits off into a new company** that immediately returns to the original company's site of origin (`splitCharacterToOrigin`, `pending-reducers.ts` — a lone character's whole company returns to origin; otherwise a new solo company is formed at the origin site; no instance lost). Reuses `play-target` `target: 'character'` and `play-condition` `requires: 'site-path'`. The generic `play-discard-cost` effect adds a play cost: the playing player must discard a card matching `filter` from `source` (`'hand'`) — the legal-action layer offers one action per (character × matching cost card) carrying `costDiscardInstanceId` (`legal-actions/movement-hazard.ts`), and the reducer (`mh-hazard-play.ts`) validates the choice against the filter, moves it to the discard pile, reveals it when `revealToOpponent` is set (`revealInstances`), and rejects the play if the cost was not paid. Used by Seized by Terror (dm-88, threshold 12, keyed to Shadow-land/Dark-domain, no cost) and Faces of the Dead (dm-57): "Playable on a non-Wizard character moving with at least two Wildernesses [{w}] in his site path if you discard any Undead hazard creature from your hand (show opponent). … If the result is less than 13, that character splits off into a different company. This new company immediately returns to his original company's site of origin." — `play-condition` `site-path` (`sitePath.wildernessCount >= 2`), `play-discard-cost` (`{ cardType: 'hazard-creature', race: 'undead' }`, revealed), `play-target` `character` (`target.race != wizard`), `seized-by-terror-check` `threshold: 13`.
- `play-discard-cost` `source: 'cards-in-play'` — the in-play-sourced sibling of the hand-sourced cost above, for a hazard **long/permanent event** whose text both requires and spends an existing in-play card. `playHazardsActions` (`legal-actions/movement-hazard.ts`, the untargeted long/permanent-event branch) offers one `play-hazard` action per own `cardsInPlay` entry matching `filter`, carrying `costDiscardInstanceId`; zero candidates makes the card unplayable outright, which doubles as a "playable only if you have … in play" gate — no separate `play-condition` needed. `mh-hazard-play.ts` pays the cost at declaration (mirroring the hand-sourced cost's timing, before the card is pushed onto the chain): it validates `costDiscardInstanceId` against the filter, then removes it from `cardsInPlay` and appends it to the owner's discard pile in the same `updatePlayer` call that clears the hand. Used by Scimitars of Steel (dm-86): "Playable only if you have a Nazgûl permanent-event in play. Discard the Nazgûl when this card is brought into play. All Orc, Troll, and Men attacks receive +1 prowess." — `play-discard-cost` (`source: 'cards-in-play'`, `filter: { keywords: { $includes: 'Nazgûl' } }`) + a plain `stat-modifier` `target: 'all-attacks'` gated `enemy.race $in [orc, troll, man]` (the as-30/dm-80 shape).
- `left-behind-split` — a hazard **short-event** the attacking (hazard) player plays *during a combat* on a non-Wizard character whose company is facing an attack of `minStrikes`+ strikes. Offered by `leftBehindActions` (`legal-actions/combat.ts`) in the attacker's `resolve-strike` Step-1 window (one `play-hazard` per non-Wizard member of the defending company, gated on `strikesPerAttack ?? strikesTotal ≥ minStrikes` and the company's remaining hazard limit). `handleLeftBehindPlay` (`combat-hazard-play.ts`) discards the card, counts it against the hazard limit (rule 8.12), and schedules a {@link PostAttackEffect} with `leftBehindSplit: true`. At combat finalization `applyLeftBehindSplit` (`combat-finalize.ts`) peels the character into a new `leftBehind` `Company` sharing the same `currentSite`/`destinationSite`/`movementPath` ("same site path", `siteCardOwned: false`, `leftBehindOriginCompanyId` set); a **lone** character instead flags his own company `leftBehindExtraPhasePending`. The new company is created *unhandled*, so the M/H loop's `select-company` gives it its own (separate) phase; `enterSetHazardLimitAndAutoAdvance` (`mh-steps.ts`) forces a `leftBehind` company's hazard-limit snapshot to **1**, and `advanceAfterCompanyMH` re-runs the lone-character company once. At the M/H→Site transition `enqueueLeftBehindRejoins`/`finalizeCompanyMH` enqueue a `left-behind-rejoin` pending resolution (merge or `pass`) for each `leftBehind` company still at the same site as its original company; `autoMergeNonHavenCompanies` skips `leftBehind` companies so the rejoin stays optional. Used by Left Behind (td-41): "Playable on a non-Wizard character whose company is facing an attack of five strikes or more. Following the attack, character splits off into a different company with the same site path as the company in which he was. He faces a separate movement/hazard phase this turn with a hazard limit of one. He may rejoin his original company following all movement/hazard phases." — `play-target` `character` (`target.race != wizard`) + `left-behind-split` (`minStrikes: 5`).
- `modify-attack` `postAttackMindRollSplit` + `split-into-own-company` — a hazard **short-event** played in the same attacker-only, pre-assignment `modify-attack` (`fromHand`) window as Unabated in Malice (ba-26), applying no stat changes of its own but scheduling a **per-character** post-attack roll: `handleModifyAttack` (`combat-actions.ts`) stores `{ threshold }` as `CombatState.mindRollSplitPending` when the effect carries `postAttackMindRollSplit`. If the attack is not fully defeated, `finalizeCombat` (`combat-finalize.ts`) enqueues one generic `dice-check` `PendingResolution` per character still in the defending company (`modifiers: [{ kind: 'constant', value: <effective mind> }]`, `comparison: 'gte'`, `onFail: { type: 'split-into-own-company' }`); the company's controller rolls each in turn. A failed roll dispatches the new `split-into-own-company` `TriggeredAction` verb (`applyDiceCheckBranch`, `pending-reducers.ts`), which delegates to `splitCharacterOffCompany` (`reducer-utils.ts`) — the generalized, auto-rejoining sibling of Left Behind's `applyLeftBehindSplit` (above): it peels the character into a new `Company` sharing the same site path, flagged `Company.forcedSoloHazardLimit` (not `leftBehind`, since the card carries no "may rejoin" clause), or flags a **lone** character's own company `Company.forcedSoloExtraPhasePending`. `enterSetHazardLimitAndAutoAdvance` (`mh-steps.ts`) forces a `forcedSoloHazardLimit` company's snapshot to 1 and **clears the flag on consumption** (unlike `leftBehind`, which persists until the explicit rejoin resolves); `advanceAfterCompanyMH` re-runs a `forcedSoloExtraPhasePending` lone company once. Because the flag is gone by the time the split company's own M/H phase ends, `autoMergeNonHavenCompanies` treats it like any other company — it auto-merges with its origin via the ordinary rule 2.IV.6 same-site merge, with no `left-behind-rejoin`-style offer. Used by Turning Hope to Despair (as-41): "Playable on a company facing a non-detainment attack from: Undead, Nazgûl, or Maia; does not count against the hazard limit. If the attack is not defeated, each character in the company makes a roll and adds his mind. If the result is less than 11, the character splits off from the company and forms his own company with the same site path as his original company. The character faces a separate movement/hazard phase this turn with a hazard limit of one." — `modify-attack` (`fromHand`, `player: 'attacker'`, `postAttackMindRollSplit: { threshold: 11 }`, `when` on `attack.detainment: false` + `enemy.race $in [undead, ringwraith, maia]`) + `play-flag: 'no-hazard-limit'`.
- `stat-modifier` `target: 'company-others'` + `organization-phase-start` attached-hazard self-discard — a `stat-modifier` scoped to **every other** character in the bearer's company (excluding the bearer). Collected in `collectCharacterEffects` (`effects/resolver.ts` `collectCompanyOthersEffects`) by scanning the attached hazards/items of every *other* company member; the effect's `when` is evaluated against the **modified** character's context. The effective-stats context now also exposes `bearer.isFollower` (true when the character is under another's direct influence — `char.controlledBy !== 'general'`, threaded through `buildEffectiveStatsContext` in `recompute-derived.ts`) so effects can exclude followers. The `reducer-untap.ts` Untap→Organization handler was extended to scan every active-player character's **attached** hazards/items/allies for `organization-phase-start` self-discard `move` effects (previously only company-bound `cardsInPlay` were scanned), evaluated against `{ bearer: { siteType, atHaven }, company: { characterCount } }` and routed to each card's **owner** via `ownerOf` (so an opponent-owned hazard returns to the opponent's pile). The character-targeting hazard play path (`legal-actions/movement-hazard.ts`) also honours a **company-scope** `duplication-limit` (counting existing copies across every company member's hazards), not just the character scope. Used by So You've Come Back (le-138): "Playable on a character of 5 mind or less. The mind of each other non-follower, non-Ringwraith, non-Wizard character in his company increases by one. Discard this card during the organization phase if target character is in a company by himself and at a Haven/Darkhaven [{H}]. Cannot be duplicated on a given company." — `play-target` `character` (`target.mind <= 5`), `stat-modifier` `mind +1` `target: 'company-others'` (`when` excludes wizard/ringwraith/fallen-wizard/balrog races + `bearer.isFollower`), `on-event` `organization-phase-start` self-discard (`company.characterCount === 1` and `bearer.atHaven`), `duplication-limit` scope `company`.
- `company-combat-boost` `companyFilter` (dual-mode combat + influence short event) — the `company-combat-boost` effect (played from hand during the defending company's `assign-strikes` pre-assignment window; applies one attack-scoped `character-stat-modifier` per boosted character) now accepts an optional `companyFilter` in addition to the per-character `filter`. `filter` restricts *which* company members receive the boost (offered when ≥1 matches, e.g. Dwarves for dm-124); `companyFilter` is a **company-level gate** — the event is offered only if ≥1 member satisfies it, and then **every** character in the company is boosted. Both the offering (`companyCombatBoostActions`, `legal-actions/combat.ts`) and the reducer (`reducer-events.ts` `handlePlayResourceShortEvent`) evaluate the gate/filter against a per-character context now including `target.keywords`. `buildPlayOptionContext` (`legal-actions/organization.ts`) also exposes `target.keywords` so the same "leader or The Balrog" gate can drive the influence-mode `play-target` filter. Used by Foe Dismayed (ba-59): "+1 prowess against an attack for all characters in a leader's or The Balrog's company **or** +3 to an influence attempt by a leader or The Balrog." — combat mode is a `company-combat-boost` (`value: 1`, `companyFilter` = `$or` [`target.keywords $includes leader`, `target.name "The Balrog"`]); influence mode reuses the Crooked Promptings (le-178) shape: `play-target` character (same `$or` filter) + `play-option influence-boost` (`when player.hasFactionInHand`, `add-constraint check-modifier influence +3`). "Balrog specific" is a deck-construction keyword, no play-time gate (ba-45/ba-46 precedent).
- `company-combat-boost` `requiredSkill` + `cost` + `costExemptRace` (cost-bearing single-target mode) — a `cost` field on a `company-combat-boost` effect switches it from "boost every `filter`-matching character" to "boost exactly one chosen character, who also pays the cost" — `filter`/`companyFilter` are ignored once `cost` is present. `companyCombatBoostActions` (`legal-actions/combat.ts`) offers one `play-short-event` per company character satisfying `requiredSkill` (checked against printed skills, mirroring `cancel-attack`'s `matchesRequirement`) that `canPayCost` accepts, carrying the chosen character as `PlayShortEventAction.targetCharacterId`. `handlePlayResourceShortEvent` (`reducer-events.ts`) resolves `action.targetCharacterId`: a cost-payer whose race matches `costExemptRace` pays nothing (same waiver semantics as `cancel-attack`'s field of the same name), otherwise `applyCost` pays the cost (e.g. enqueues a corruption check via `cost-evaluator.ts`), then the attack-scoped `character-stat-modifier` constraint is added for that one character only. Used by Some Secret Art of Flame (le-232): "Playable on a sorcery-using character facing an attack. +4 prowess for the character against the attack. Unless he is a Ringwraith, character makes a corruption check modified by -4. Cannot be duplicated against a given attack." — `requiredSkill: "sorcery"`, `cost: { check: "corruption", modifier: -4 }`, `costExemptRace: "ringwraith"`, plus a `duplication-limit` scope `attack`.

- `company-combat-boost` `requiredRace` (race-gated cost-bearing single-target mode) — a sibling to `requiredSkill` (same cost-bearing single-target mode) that gates the one qualifying/boosted/paying character by printed `race` instead of skill. `companyCombatBoostActions` (`legal-actions/combat.ts`) skips a character whose `race` doesn't match `requiredRace` when the cost-bearing branch enumerates candidates; the reducer needs no change since it trusts `targetCharacterId` chosen by the legal-action layer, mirroring how `requiredSkill` is only checked at offer time. Used by Wizard's Fire (tw-360): "Spell. Wizard only. +5 prowess for the Wizard against one attack. Wizard makes a corruption check modified by -4." — `requiredRace: "wizard"`, `cost: { check: "corruption", modifier: -4 }` (no `costExemptRace` — the check always applies).
- `company-overt` ally + `bearer-company-moves` movement-type discard + `organization-phase-start` opponent elimination roll — a Spawn ally whose controlling character's company is overt (existing `company-overt`), that self-discards only on certain movement kinds, and that penalises a non-Balrog controller each organization phase. The `bearer-company-moves` on-event context (`mh-hazard-play.ts` step 8a-2) now also exposes `movementType` (`starter`/`region`/`special`/`under-deeps`) so a self-discard `move` can gate on it (`when: { movementType: { $in: ["region", "starter"] } }`). The `organization-phase-start` attached-card scan (`reducer-untap.ts`) now exposes the host character's `bearer.name`/`bearer.mind` and, beyond a self-discard `move`, supports the `enqueue-opponent-elimination-roll` apply — it enqueues a generic `dice-check` (roller/actor = the active player's opponent, `threshold = bearer.mind`, `comparison: "gt"`, one `constant` `modifier`, `onPass: { type: "eliminate-character" }`) scoped to the Organization phase. The new `eliminate-character` dice-check branch (`pending-reducers.ts` `applyDiceCheckBranch` → `eliminateCharacter`, a wrapper over the generalized `discardCharacter` with the character card routed to its owner's out-of-play pile) removes the character from its company, eliminates it, discards its possessions and frees its followers. Used by Evil Things Lingering (ba-45): "Playable at The Under-galleries, The Under-courts, or Remains of Thangorodrim. Its controlling character's company is overt. Discard this ally if its company moves using region or starter movement. If this ally's controlling character is not The Balrog, your opponent makes a roll during your organization phase and subtracts four. The controlling character is eliminated if the result is greater than his mind." — `company-overt`, `on-event` `bearer-company-moves` (`when movementType $in [region, starter]`) self-discard, `on-event` `organization-phase-start` (`when bearer.name $ne "The Balrog"`) `enqueue-opponent-elimination-roll` (`modifier: -4`).
- `counter-cancel-attack-roll` + `modify-attack` (dual-mode Spider hazard short-event) — a hazard short-event the **attacking** (hazard) player plays during a combat chain to counter an opponent's chain entry that would cancel a matching-race attack, roll-gated. Offered by `counterCancelRollChainActions` (`legal-actions/chain.ts`) while a chain is active + `state.combat` exists, the acting player is the attacker, `combat.creatureRace` ∈ the effect's `race`, and an unresolved opponent entry carries a `cancel-attack` effect; sources are the attacker's hand **plus** any unrevealed on-guard cards on the defending company ("may be revealed as an on-guard card"). The `counter-cancel-roll` action (routed via `handleChainAction`/`reducer.ts` dispatch list) discards the card and pushes it onto the chain as a short-event entry carrying a **dedicated** `counterCancelTargetInstanceId` payload field (not `targetInstanceId`, which triggers the Twilight environment-cancel path). Sitting above the cancel entry (LIFO) it resolves first: `resolveEntry` (`chain-reducer.ts`) enqueues a generic `dice-check` (roll 2d6 + the attack's current `combat.strikeProwess`, `comparison: "gt"`, `threshold`); on success the new `counter-cancel-attack` dice-check onPass verb (`applyDiceCheckBranch`, `pending-reducers.ts`) negates the targeted cancel entry (attack survives) and adds `prowessBonus` to `combat.strikeProwess`, on failure the cancel resolves and ends the attack; the check's `continuation` (`chain-entry` match `source`) marks the counter entry resolved and resumes the chain. Used by Black Vapour (ba-14): counter a Spider-attack cancel (roll + attack prowess > 14 → negate + 1 prowess), plus the alternative "+1 prowess to a Spider attack" as a plain `modify-attack` (`fromHand`, `player: attacker`, `when enemy.race $in [spider, spiders]`) which also carries the on-guard reveal for that mode.
- `attacker-attack-option` — an **optional, per-attack** combat modifier granted to the controller of an in-play card (the attacking / hazard player): for each attack whose `combat.creatureRace` equals the effect's `creatureRace`, the controller may choose to apply `prowessModifier` (added to `combat.strikeProwess`, every strike) and/or make the attack `detainment`. Offered as an `apply-attacker-attack-option` combat action in the attacker's `resolve-strike` Step 1 window (CoE rule 3.iv.1) before any strike has resolved — so the modifier affects the whole attack — and only once per attack (flagged `CombatState.attackerAttackOptionApplied`). The controller may simply pass (decline). Legal actions in `legal-actions/combat.ts` (`attackerAttackOptionActions`, wired into the resolve-strike branch and the Step 1 attacker-priority gate); reducer `handleApplyAttackerAttackOption` (`reducer-combat.ts`). Used by Ungoliant's Progeny (ba-27): "The Wind-deeps and The Rusted-deeps each have an additional automatic-attack: Spawn — 2 strikes with 16/8. In addition, for each Spider attack your opponent faces, you can choose for it to be at +1 prowess and detainment." — `permanent-event-auto-attack` (`onDefeat: "remove-from-play"`, Spawn 2×16/8 at ba-104/ba-96) + `attacker-attack-option` (`creatureRace: "spider"`, `prowessModifier: 1`, `detainment: true`).
- `grant-replay-attacked-creature` (Spawn permanent-event) + `permanent-event-auto-attack` `onDefeat: "remove-from-play"` for a kill-MP Spawn — an in-play hazard permanent-event that (a) augments named Under-deeps sites with an extra automatic-attack and (b) grants its controller a once-per-company-M/H-phase "replay" of a filtered creature from their own discard pile. During the M/H play-hazards window, `spawnReplayCreatureFromDiscardActions` (`legal-actions/movement-hazard.ts`) walks the hazard player's `cardsInPlay` for the effect and offers a `spawn-replay-creature` per (discard creature matching `filter` whose name is in `hazardsEncountered`, keying-match); unlike Exhalation of Decay's hand-driven `play-creature-from-discard`, this replay **counts against the hazard limit** and is limited to once per source per company M/H phase (tracked in the new `MovementHazardPhaseState.spawnReplayUsedSources`, reset alongside `hazardsEncountered`). The reducer `handleSpawnReplayCreature` (`mh-hazard-play.ts`) validates the gate, removes the creature from discard, counts the hazard, records the source, and initiates the creature chain. The "This card must have already attacked the company this turn" gate is read (per the French text) as the *replayed creature* having attacked this M/H phase — the only temporally reachable reading, since site auto-attacks resolve in the site phase after the hazard window. The companion `onDefeat: "remove-from-play"` on the card's `permanent-event-auto-attack` realises CoE rule 964 ("hazard events that create an attack and have a marshalling point value designated as kill points" go to the defender's MP pile when their attack is defeated): defeating the augmented Spawn attack moves the card to the defeating player's kill pile, awarding its kill MPs (`finalizeCombat`, the same block as Balrog of Moria tw-12). Used by Monstrosity of Diverse Shape (ba-21): Unique Spawn, +Spawn auto-attack (2 strikes 15/9) at The Drowning-deeps (ba-89) / Remains of Thangorodrim (ba-95), 4 kill MP, and the once-per-turn Wolf/Animal discard replay (`filter` `race $in [wolves, animals]`).
- `play-creature-from-discard` `bodyModifier` — extends the discard-pile immediate-attack short-event (Exhalation of Decay dm-55, prowess-only) with an optional body modifier alongside `prowessModifier`. A new `ChainEntryPayload` (`type: 'creature'`) field `bodyBonus` carries it from `handlePlayCreatureFromDiscard` (`mh-hazard-play.ts`) to `chain-reducer.ts`'s creature combat-initiation block, where it is added to `effectiveBody` (clamped to a minimum of 0, mirroring the existing `combat-body-per-defender-skill` clamp) alongside the pre-existing `prowessBonus`/`strikesBonus` fields (Fell Beast tw-33). Used by In Great Wrath (dm-66): "Playable on a Nazgûl in your discard pile that could immediately attack. The Nazgûl attacks immediately (not counting against the hazard limit) with +2 prowess and -1 body." — `play-creature-from-discard` (`filter: { race: "ringwraith" }`, `prowessModifier: 2`, `bodyModifier: -1`); "that could immediately attack" reuses the same standard creature-keying check as dm-55's "if target Undead can attack".
- `company-site-phase-do-nothing` + `play-target: "company"` short-event filter — a hazard short-event played on the active M/H company that forbids it from doing anything during its upcoming **site phase this turn** (a `site-phase-do-nothing` constraint is added to the company via `applyCompanySitePhaseDoNothing` in `chain-reducer.ts` — the same constraint `company-return-to-origin` uses — but the company keeps its destination site and its movement is unaffected). Playability rides a companion `play-target: "company"` filter: the short-event company-target path (`legal-actions/movement-hazard.ts`) exposes, for the active company (destination site if moving, else current site), `target.siteType`, `target.siteKeywords`, `target.characterCount` (allies excluded), `target.spawnInPlayCount` (`countSpawnCardsInPlay` in `reducer-utils.ts` — cards carrying the `spawn` keyword in any `cardsInPlay`, Spawn characters in companies, and Spawn allies attached to characters; discard/elimination piles are not counted), and the precomputed `target.moreSpawnThanCompany` boolean (`spawnInPlayCount > characterCount`, since `$gt` compares a field to a literal, not two fields). Used by Darkness Made by Malice (ba-15): "Playable on a company at or moving to a Ruins & Lairs [{R}] or Under-deeps site, if there are more Spawn cards in play than characters in the company. Eliminated Spawn do not count. The company must do nothing during its site phase this turn." — `play-target` `company` (`$and[ $or[ target.siteType == ruins-and-lairs, target.siteKeywords $includes under-deeps ], target.moreSpawnThanCompany ]`) + `company-site-phase-do-nothing`.
- `item-play-corruption-check` — a hazard **short-event** played on a company's site (`play-target: site`) that installs a turn-scoped `item-play-corruption-check` {@link ActiveConstraint} bound to that site (threaded via the short-event chain payload's new `targetSiteDefinitionId`; installed in `chain-reducer.ts` `applyItemPlayCorruptionCheckConstraint`, targeting the resource/item-playing player). During the site phase, when an item is played at the bound site, `fireItemPlayCorruptionChecks` (`reducer-site.ts`, hooked next to `fireCharacterGainsItemChecks` in the item-play path) enqueues one corruption check per character in the company **except** the character playing the item and any character matching the effect's `exemptFilter` (evaluated against `{ target: { race, skills, name } }`). Each check's modifier is the negative of the item's printed `corruptionPoints`. Per CRF 22 the trigger fires only on item *play* (including a special ring item), never on *transfer* (which does not route through the item-play handler). "Cannot be duplicated on a given site" is a `duplication-limit` scope `site` handled in the hazard short-event dup-limit block (`legal-actions/movement-hazard.ts`), which counts the resolved copy's site-bound constraint. Used by Greed (le-113 / tw-42): "Playable on a site. Until the end of the turn, each non-Hobbit, non-Wizard, non-Ringwraith character at the site must make a corruption check each time an item is played at the site … modified by subtracting the corruption points the item would normally give the character." — `play-target` site, `duplication-limit` scope site, `item-play-corruption-check` (`exemptFilter` `target.race $in [hobbit, wizard, ringwraith]`).
- `character-stat-modifier` from a fixed (non-`play-option`) hazard short-event + `play-condition` `requires: 'company-site'` — a character-targeting hazard short-event may apply turn-scoped `character-stat-modifier` constraints without a player choice: `applyShortEventSelfEntersPlayConstraints` (`chain-reducer.ts`) now, for each `on-event: self-enters-play` → `add-constraint` `character-stat-modifier` effect, targets the short-event's chosen character (`targetCharacterId`) and evaluates the effect's optional `when` against `{ inPlay: [...card names...] }`, so a *doubled* modifier can be gated on a companion card. The stacked constraints sum in the effective-stats resolver, and a `duplication-limit` `scope: "turn"` (counting active constraints from a resolved copy) enforces "cannot be duplicated on a given turn". The new `play-condition` `requires: 'company-site'` gates such a hazard on the active company's **relevant site** (its `destinationSite` when moving, else `currentSite`) via a generic DSL `condition` over `{ site: { name, siteType, region, keywords } }`, checked in the play-hazards block of `legal-actions/movement-hazard.ts`. Used by Glance of Arien (ba-19): "Environment. Playable on The Balrog at or moving to a non-Under-deeps site. -2/-1 to his prowess/body until the end of the turn. This modification is -4/-2 if Gates of Morning is in play. Cannot be duplicated on a given turn." — `play-target` character (`target.name: "The Balrog"`), `play-condition` `company-site` (`{ $not: { site.keywords $includes under-deeps } }`), `duplication-limit` scope turn, two base `character-stat-modifier` self-enters-play effects (prowess -2, body -1) plus two more gated `when: { inPlay: "Gates of Morning" }`.
- `site-instance-transform` + `conditional-mp` — a kept resource permanent-event bound to a site (`attachedToSite`) that **splits** a site's effective type and automatic-attacks between the one instance it is attached to ("the associated site" = the controller's own current site) and every other in-play copy of the same site definition ("all other versions"). Unlike the generic `site-type-override` `attribute-modifier` (as-88), it **bypasses the MEAS §6(d) Under-deeps type-immutability short-circuit** and discriminates by *instance*: both `getEffectiveSiteType` (`engine/effective.ts`) and `getActiveAutoAttacks` (`engine/manifestations.ts`) take an optional site **instance** id (threaded through every site-phase/movement-destination/haven caller in `reducer-site.ts`, `combat-finalize.ts`, `chain-reducer.ts`, `reducer-untap.ts`, and the `legal-actions/*` site/movement/org paths), and the shared `resolveSiteInstanceTransform` decides `associated` vs `other` by whether that instance is the current site of one of the carrying card's controller's companies. `associated.removeAllAutoAttacks` strips every automatic-attack from the Darkhaven copy; `others.addAutoAttack` appends one to every other version. The transform is dormant while the card is `pendingTriggerAttack` (before its keep is confirmed). The bound site is permanent — exempt from the site-attached orphan sweep (`discardOrphanedSiteAttachedEvents`) and always returned to the owner's location deck rather than discarded (`mh-hazard-play.ts` step-8 `cavernsBound`), the same treatment as `surface-region-adjacency` (ba-51). The companion `conditional-mp` effect (`bonus`, `requiresCardOnSameSite`) adds a flat bonus to the carrying card's printed marshalling points while a named card is in play attached to the same site (folded into the `cardsInPlay` MP tally in `recompute-derived.ts`). Used by Roots of the Earth (ba-74): "Playable during the site phase on an Under-deeps site normally a Ruins & Lairs [{R}] if The Balrog is there. The Balrog's company faces an attack (Drake — 2 strikes with 13 prowess). Following the attack, tap The Balrog or discard this card. The associated site is a Darkhaven [{H}] and loses all automatic-attacks. All other versions of the site become a Shadow-hold [{S}] and gain an additional automatic-attack: Orcs — 5 strikes with 9 prowess. If Breach the Hold is on the same site, this card gives 3 marshalling points. This site is never discarded or returned to its location deck. Cannot be duplicated on a given site." — `play-target` site (`under-deeps` + `ruins-and-lairs`) + `play-target` character (`target.name: "The Balrog"`, restricting the keep) + `duplication-limit` scope site + `trigger-attack-on-play` (1× Drake 2/13, `move-to-mp-pile`) + `site-instance-transform` + `conditional-mp` (+2, "Breach the Hold"), printed MP 1.
- `site-type-override` with `purpose: "healing"` (healing-only haven override) — a permanent resource-event played on a Free-hold (`play-target` site, `filter.siteType: "free-hold"`) that makes the site count as a Haven **for healing only**. The `self-enters-play` → `add-constraint site-type-override` (`overrideType: "haven"`, `purpose: "healing"`, `scope: "until-cleared"`) resolves through `constraint-kind.ts` to an `attribute-modifier` `site.type override → haven` carrying a new `healingOnly: true` marker. The untap-phase healing sweep (`reducer-untap.ts`) scans the constraint directly and honours it (wounded characters at the bound site heal to Tapped), but `getEffectiveSiteType` (`engine/effective.ts`) **skips** `healingOnly` overrides — so the general effective site type stays the printed Free-hold, leaving hazard keying, movement, bring-into-play, and item/faction/ally playability untouched (contrast the full `site-type-override` used by The White Tree tw-348 / Hold Rebuilt and Repaired as-88). The card binds to the site (`attachedToSite`, via the play action's `targetSiteDefinitionId`) and is discarded — with its constraint cleared — by the existing site-attached orphan sweep (`discardOrphanedSiteAttachedEvents`) once no company occupies the bound site. Used by Houses of Healing (td-125): "Playable on a Free-hold [{F}]. Site becomes a Haven [{H}] for the purposes of healing. Discard Houses of Healing when the site is returned to the location deck or discarded." — `play-target` site (`siteType: "free-hold"`) + `on-event self-enters-play` → `site-type-override` (`overrideType: "haven"`, `purpose: "healing"`).
- `site-type-override` with `purpose: "healing-and-character-play"` (Mallorn dm-148) — the inverse of `healing-and-hazards` (The White Tree tw-348): the override is honoured for healing and for "bringing characters into play" only, hidden from every other `getEffectiveSiteType` consumer (hazard keying, movement, storage, item/faction/ally playability). `constraint-kind.ts` maps this `purpose` to a new `characterPlayOnly: true` marker on the `attribute-modifier`. `getEffectiveSiteType` (`engine/effective.ts`) skips a `characterPlayOnly` override whenever its caller passes `excludeCharacterPlayOverrides: false` (every general consumer's default) and only honours it when the caller passes `true` — i.e. exactly the two character-recruiting call sites (`legal-actions/organization-characters.ts`'s haven-for-recruiting check, `legal-actions/recruit-via-event.ts`). The untap-phase healing sweep (`reducer-untap.ts`) scans `site.type` override constraints directly rather than through `getEffectiveSiteType`, so it honours the override unconditionally, exactly like a plain `healingOnly` override. Also new: the **`card-stored-at-site`** `play-condition` (`legal-actions/site.ts`, alongside `card-attached-to-site`) gates a site-phase permanent-event on the playing player's marshalling-point pile (`killPile`) holding a card named `cardName` stamped `storedAtSite` with the active company's current site's definition id — the *stored-item* counterpart of `card-attached-to-site`'s in-play attachment check. And **`site-untaps-during-untap-phase`** / **`site-always-returns-to-deck`**, two new `site-flag` constraint kinds (the same `{ siteDefinitionId }` collapsed-primitive family as `skip-automatic-attacks` / `cancel-attacks-at-site`): the first is consulted by a new sweep in `performUntap` (`reducer-untap.ts`) that untaps a tapped `company.currentSite` carrying the flag — defying CoE's "site cards don't untap" rule — and the second is OR-ed into the `alwaysReturnToDeck` check at M/H step 8 (`mh-hazard-play.ts`), the dynamic counterpart of the printed `always-return-to-deck` site-rule (Buhr Widu td-173) for a site that only gains the behaviour while a permanent event is in play there. Finally, `cardKeepsBoundSitePermanent` (`reducer-utils.ts`) now also recognises a card carrying a `site-always-returns-to-deck` add-constraint effect as keeping its bound site permanent — exempting it from the site-attached orphan sweep, since a site that always returns to the location deck is never truly out of circulation (contrast Houses of Healing td-125, which *is* swept once its site is unoccupied). Used by Mallorn (dm-148): "Unique. Playable at Bag End only if Earth of Galadriel's Orchard is stored there. Bag End becomes a Haven [{H}] for the purposes of healing and bringing characters into play. Bag End can untap during its owner's untap phase. If Bag End is discarded, return it to its location deck. All Hobbit factions are worth +1 marshalling points." — `play-target` site (`name: "Bag End"`) + `play-condition card-stored-at-site` (`cardName: "Earth of Galadriel's Orchard"`) + three `on-event self-enters-play` → `add-constraint` effects (`site-type-override` `purpose: "healing-and-character-play"`, `site-untaps-during-untap-phase`, `site-always-returns-to-deck`) + `faction-mp-bonus` (`bonus: 1`, `races: ["hobbit"]`, no gate — the first ungated user of the primitive introduced by Alliance of Free Peoples as-45). Earth of Galadriel's Orchard (tw-221) was additionally given the `storable-at` effect ("2 marshalling points if stored at Bag End") its own printed text always called for, so the combo is actually reachable in play; its own remaining restriction ("Only playable at Lórien") is unrelated to this card and left for tw-221's own certification.
- `evil-hour-tap-trigger` + `evil-hour-grant-movement` (+ `play-flag: no-auto-untap`) — a Balrog permanent-event whose usability gates on a passive tap trigger and whose payoff is a persistent conditional region-movement grant. **(1) Tap trigger** — `applyEvilHourTaps` (`engine/evil-hour.ts`), wired into `postReduce` with the pre-action state, runs after every reducer step: for each player controlling an untapped in-play card with an `evil-hour-tap-trigger` effect, it diffs the **opponent's** in-play scoring instance-ids between the pre- and post-action state (bare `cardsInPlay`, every company character, and their items/allies) and — when a freshly-played card's printed `marshallingPoints` ≥ `mpThreshold` — taps every untapped copy of the carrying card (idempotent; scoped to the opponent so the controller's own plays never trigger it). "This card does not untap" reuses the existing `play-flag: no-auto-untap` (honoured in `reducer-untap.ts`). **(2) Grant** — while the card is **tapped**, `evilHourGrantMovementActions` (`legal-actions/organization.ts`) offers a `discard-for-evil-hour-movement` per (source × eligible non-empty company that may region-move, i.e. `!companyContainsBalrogAvatar`); the reducer (`reducer-organization.ts` `handleDiscardForEvilHourMovement`) discards the card and sets the persistent `Company.evilHourMovementBonus` (a company field, *not* reset at turn boundaries, unlike `extraRegionDistance`). `evilHourRegionBonus` then adds +2 region distance whenever the marked company moves **to** or **from** a site currently holding an opponent's company (`siteHasOpponentCompany`, matched by site name) — applied at both the org-phase plan-movement pass (`legal-actions/organization-companies.ts`, extending the reachable set to opponent-occupied destinations, or all destinations when the origin is opponent-occupied) and the M/H declare-path region cap (`legal-actions/movement-hazard.ts`; never when Out He Sprang has fixed the allowance). Used by A More Evil Hour (ba-48): "Tap this card when an opponent plays a card normally giving him three or more marshalling points. This card does not untap. If tapped, you may discard this card during your organization phase to target a company allowed to move with region movement. The company may move up to two additional regions if moving to a site where an opponent's company is present — and also, thereafter, when leaving this site." — `play-flag no-auto-untap` + `evil-hour-tap-trigger` (`mpThreshold: 3`) + `evil-hour-grant-movement` (`extraRegions: 2`). The "and also, thereafter, when leaving this site" clause is read as the symmetric reading (bonus on a move whose origin **or** destination holds an opponent's company). "Balrog specific" is a deck-construction keyword with no play-time gate (ba-45/ba-46 precedent).
- **Dual "Permanent-event/Short-event" resource card** (`check-modifier` `target: "player-in-play"` + `conditional-mp` `requiresFactionCount` + `reshuffle-from-discard` `altShortEventMode`) — an `eventType: "permanent"` resource card offered **both** as a permanent-event and as a resource short-event. (1) The **player-scoped ongoing influence bonus** is a bare `check-modifier` (`check: "influence"`, `target: "player-in-play"`) borne by the card sitting in the influencing player's `cardsInPlay` (not attached to anything); `collectPlayerInPlayInfluenceEffects` (`reducer-utils.ts`) collects it — pre-filtering the effect's `when` against the faction-influence resolver context — at both the display (`legal-actions/site.ts`) and the roll resolver (`reducer-site.ts`), so the bonus lasts exactly while the card stays in play (contrast the fixed-scope ba-78 constraint form). (2) The **conditional-MP** gate `conditional-mp` gains a `requiresFactionCount` variant (`{ min, races, unique?, excludePlayableAtSiteType? }`): the bonus applies while the controller has ≥`min` `races` factions in their own `cardsInPlay`, counting only unique ones when set and excluding factions playable at a site of `excludePlayableAtSiteType` (a named site's type resolved from the pool via `factionPlayableAtSiteType`) — evaluated in `recompute-derived.ts` (`conditionalMpFactionCount`); the printed `marshallingPoints` is 0, so the card scores only via this bonus. (3) The **alternative short-event mode** is a `reshuffle-from-discard` flagged `altShortEventMode: true` (`scope: "self"`): `altShortEventReshuffleEffect` (`reducer-utils.ts`) makes `heroResourceShortEventActions` (`long-event.ts`) and `playResourceShortEventActions` (`organization.ts`) admit the permanent-typed card as a `play-short-event`, viable only when `playerHasReshuffleMatch` finds a matching card in the discard; `handlePlayResourceShortEvent` resolves the reshuffle (matched Orc/Troll factions discard→deck) and discards the card, leaving the permanent-event mode untouched. "Cannot be duplicated as a permanent-event" is `duplication-limit` scope game (permanent-event path only). Used by Great Army of the North (ba-38): "As a permanent-event, +1 to your influence attempts against Orc and Troll factions. If you have at least 4 unique Orc and/or Troll factions —none playable at a Darkhold [{D}]—you receive this card's marshalling points. Cannot be duplicated as a permanent-event. Alternatively, as a short-event, you may choose any Orc and Troll factions from your discard pile and shuffle them into your play deck."

- `play-condition: card-attached-to-site` / `card-on-adjacent-under-deeps` + `trigger-attack-on-play.discardUniqueFactionsAtSite` + `site-instance-transform` extensions (`removeAutoAttacksByRace`, `noFactions`) — the "Balrog usurps a Dwarf-hold" combo of a permanent-event played **on another in-play permanent-event**. **(1) Play-conditions** (`legal-actions/site.ts`, permanent-event block): `card-attached-to-site` (`cardName`) requires a kept (`!pendingTriggerAttack`) card of that name attached (`attachedToSite`) to the active company's current site in either player's `cardsInPlay`; `card-on-adjacent-under-deeps` (`cardName`) requires such a card attached to an **Under-deeps site whose `adjacentSites` names the current site** (looked up in `state.cardPool`, gated on the `under-deeps` keyword). **(2) Faction discard** — `trigger-attack-on-play.discardUniqueFactionsAtSite` threads a new flag (combat-finalize.ts / combat-cancel.ts → `select-card-bearer` pending kind) so the `move-to-mp-pile` keep discards every **unique** faction in play (either player) playable at the company's current site to its **owner's** discard pile (`applySelectCardBearerResolution`, `pending-reducers.ts`) — distinct from `discardFactionsAtSite` (active player, all factions) and `returnFactionsAtSite` (returns unique to hand). **(3) Transform extensions** — `SiteInstanceTransformEffect.associated.removeAutoAttacksByRace` / `others.removeAutoAttacksByRace` filter out automatic-attacks of a named creature race (matched on `creatureType`) in `getActiveAutoAttacks` (before `others.addAutoAttack` is appended), and a top-level `noFactions` bars every faction play at any version of the transformed site (checked via `resolveSiteInstanceTransform` in the `legal-actions/site.ts` faction branch). Used by Invade Their Domain (ba-64): "Playable during the site phase on the Blue Mountain or Iron Hill Dwarf-hold if The Balrog is there and Breach the Hold is on its adjacent Under-deeps site. The Balrog's company faces 3 attacks (Dwarves — 5/9, 4/10, 3/12). Following the attacks, tap The Balrog or discard this card. If this card is not discarded, discard all unique factions playable at the site. … Cannot be duplicated on a given site." — `play-target` site (`name $in [Blue Mountain Dwarf-hold, Iron Hill Dwarf-hold]`) + `play-target` character (`target.name: "The Balrog"`) + `play-condition card-on-adjacent-under-deeps` ("Breach the Hold") + `duplication-limit` scope site + `trigger-attack-on-play` (3× Dwarves, `move-to-mp-pile`, `discardUniqueFactionsAtSite`); no permanence exemption, so the site sweep discards it. And by Lord and Usurper (ba-65): "Playable during the site phase on Invade Their Domain. The company faces 2 attacks (Dwarves — 4/9, 3/10). Following the attacks, tap a character or discard this card. If this card is not discarded, discard all unique factions playable at the site. All versions of the associated site become a Shadow-hold [{S}], may have no factions played there, and lose all Dwarf automatic-attacks. Other versions gain an automatic-attack: Orcs — 4/7. Cannot be duplicated on a given card." — `play-target` site (same Dwarf-holds) + `play-condition card-attached-to-site` ("Invade Their Domain") + `duplication-limit` scope site + `trigger-attack-on-play` (2× Dwarves, `move-to-mp-pile`, `discardUniqueFactionsAtSite`, **no** character `play-target` so any untapped member may keep) + `site-instance-transform` (`associated`/`others` → shadow-hold, both `removeAutoAttacksByRace: "Dwarves"`, `others.addAutoAttack` Orcs 4/7, `noFactions`).
- `site-rule: dynamic-under-deeps-adjacency` — an Under-deeps site whose printed adjacency is *chosen when it is played* rather than fixed on the card ("one Under-deeps <type> chosen by you when playing this card (N)"). Since the engine has no unoccupied-in-play site zone in which to record the once-chosen connection, the faithful generalization is to treat the site as Under-deeps-adjacent — at the rule's `roll` — to any **other** Under-deeps site whose (printed) effective type is one of `siteTypes`; the connected site must carry the `under-deeps` keyword, so no *surface* site is ever adjacent ("no surface site"). Symmetric and player-agnostic (`dynamicUnderDeepsAdjacencyRoll` in `legal-actions/organization-companies.ts`), wired into `isUnderDeepsAdjacent` (org-phase plan-movement / M/H declare-path reachability) and `getUnderDeepsRequiredRoll` (`mh-steps.ts`). Used by Ancient Deep-hold (ba-83): "no surface site, one Under-deeps Ruins & Lairs [{R}] chosen by you when playing this card (8)" — reachable from, and back to, any Under-deeps Ruins & Lairs at roll 8, and never a Dark-hold Under-deeps site nor a surface Ruins & Lairs. Its remaining rules reuse shipped primitives: 3 fixed Undead automatic-attacks (data); "Each character wounded must make a corruption check modified by -2" is the `on-event: character-wounded-by-self` → `force-check` (modifier -2) machinery (le-353 Barrow-downs), which fires per wounded character in `combat-finalize.ts` for a site automatic-attack (its `getAttackSourceCard` resolves to the site def); "Any Undead and Spider creatures may be keyed to this site" is two `allow-creature-by-race` rules (`race` "undead"/"spider"); and "This site is never discarded or returned to its location deck" is `site-rule: always-return-to-deck` (M/H step 8 always returns the site to the owner's location deck, never the discard pile).
- `site-rule: allow-creature-by-race` (with optional `except` condition) — a site whose text reads "Any <race> creature (except <named>) may be keyed to this site." The rule grants a keying bypass to any hazard creature whose `race` matches the rule's `race` and does not match the optional `except` condition, regardless of the creature's printed keying. Shared by the normal M/H hazard-creature keying path (`siteAllowsCreatureByRace` in `legal-actions/movement-hazard.ts`) and the `dynamic-auto-attack` 2nd-attack eligibility check (`legal-actions/site.ts`); the latter only honours the bypass when the dynamic attack keys by **site-type** (being keyable to this site is a form of site keying). In the shared card data the great named Dragons carry `race: "dragon"` (Smaug, Scatha, Cave-drake tw-020, …) while lesser drakes carry `race: "drake"`, so "any Dragon" and "any Drake" are distinct rules. Precedent: Geann a-Lisch (as-138, "any Man"), The Iron-deeps (ba-91, "any Drake except Sea Serpent"), The Rusted-deeps (ba-96, "any Dragon except Eärcaraxë"), The Gem-deeps (ba-90 balrog / as-147 minion, "any Undead or Pûkel-creature" — paired with the `dynamic-auto-attack` {S} 2nd attack; as-147 was a pure data-completion certification mirroring ba-90 with minion draws 2/3 and Pûkel-deeps adjacency 9).
- `site-rule: allow-items-when-tapped` — a site whose text reads "Items may be played at this site even if it is tapped." The rule bypasses only the site-tap precondition on the item-play branch (`legal-actions/site.ts`, `allowWhenTapped` in the `isItemCard` block): items the site lists in `playableResources` stay playable while the site is tapped, but the allowed subtypes are unchanged and playing the item still follows the normal tap-site/one-resource flow. Alignment-agnostic. Precedent: Tharbad hero td-180; also Tharbad minion le-407 (whose imported `playableResources` was empty against the printed "Playable: Items (minor)" — data fix to `["minor"]`, same import bug as Stone-circle le-405).
- `site-rule: allow-agent-play` — a site whose text lets **agent** characters be brought into play there under a controlling character's **direct influence**, overriding rule 2.II.2.2.5 (which otherwise confines an agent played as a character to its own home site). Consumed by `legal-actions/organization-characters.ts`: `siteAllowsAgentPlay` widens `findPlayableSites` so an agent may join a company already at the site, and the site is flagged `directInfluenceOnly` so the general-influence branch is skipped (only the DI-follower branch offers the play). Alignment gating is unchanged — only Ringwraith/Fallen-wizard players may play agents as characters at all (Wizard/Balrog treat them as hazards), so the permission never bypasses rule 1.3.W2/1.3.B2. The reducer trusts the legal-action gate; agent play at the site executes as a normal `play-character` with `controlledBy` = the controller instance. Used by Bree (le-356): "Agent minions may be brought into play under direct influence at this site." Its other rules reuse shipped primitives: a single Dúnedain each-character automatic-attack (data, prowess 7) that is `combat-detainment` gated on `defender.covert` (the Minas Tirith le-391 "detainment against covert company" encoding).
- `eddy-lock` — a Balrog site-phase permanent-event that binds a site (`attachedToSite` = the site *definition* id) and locks it. Played via the site-phase permanent-event path (`legal-actions/site.ts`) with no trigger-attack: `play-flag untapped-site-required` (untapped site) + `play-flag tap-site-on-play` (taps the site on play) + a `play-target: site` filter excluding Under-deeps sites and their surfaces (`$not keywords $includes under-deeps` + `isUnderDeepsSurface $ne true`, the ba-77 machinery) + a `play-condition: company-context` requiring The Balrog in the company (`company.characterNames $includes "The Balrog"`). On resolution the `eddy-lock` handler in `chain-reducer.ts` taps The Balrog in the active company (a play cost, not the attachment — the card attaches to the site). Three ongoing effects: **(1) permanence** — the card is exempt from the site-attached orphan sweep and the bound site is always returned to the owner's location deck (never discarded) when a company leaves it, via `cardKeepsBoundSitePermanent` (`reducer-utils.ts`), the same treatment as `surface-region-adjacency` ("This site is never discarded"); **(2) never untaps for the owner** — when the Eddy owner's company moves to a version of the bound site definition, the destination is placed **tapped** rather than untapped (`mh-hazard-play.ts` step 8, gated by `siteEddyLock(state, destDef, movingPlayer)`), realising "never untaps for you" (the engine never untaps a stationary site, so re-placement on movement is the only refresh point); **(3) two-character tax** — any company (either player) at any version of the bound site definition must tap `taxTapCharacters` of its characters during its site phase before it may play an ally or item there. `siteEddyLock` (`reducer-utils.ts`, scans both players' `cardsInPlay` for an `eddy-lock` card whose `attachedToSite` matches the site definition) drives the gate: `SitePhaseState.eddyTaxTapped` tracks how many have been paid this site phase (reset per company), the item and ally play paths in `legal-actions/site.ts` are barred with a "must tap two characters first" reason while the count is below `taxTapCharacters`, and a `pay-site-tax` action (one per untapped company member) taps one character and increments the count (`reducer-site.ts`). Used by Eddy in Fate's Tide (ba-57): "Playable during the site phase on an untapped site if The Balrog is there; the site cannot be an Under-deeps site or surface site thereof. Tap The Balrog and the site. This site is never discarded and never untaps for you. Before a company can play any ally or item at any version of this site, it must tap two characters during the site phase." — `play-flag untapped-site-required` + `play-flag tap-site-on-play` + `play-target: site` (not under-deeps / not surface) + `play-condition: company-context` (The Balrog present) + `eddy-lock` (`taxTapCharacters` 2). ("Balrog specific" is a deck-construction keyword with no play-time gate.)
- `site-lock` — a generic, tax-free Balrog site-domination lock, the sibling of `eddy-lock`. Carried by a **`trigger-attack-on-play`** permanent-event played on the untapped site (`play-target: site`) and *kept* in the marshalling-point pile (`afterAttack: "move-to-mp-pile"`), so the card stays bound (`attachedToSite` = the site definition id). It taps no character on play (the site is tapped by `play-flag: tap-site-on-play`) and levies no per-company tax. While in play and no longer `pendingTriggerAttack`, three ongoing effects fire: **(1) permanence** — recognised by the shared `cardKeepsBoundSitePermanent` predicate (`reducer-utils.ts`), like `eddy-lock` / `surface-region-adjacency` ("This site is never discarded"): exempt from the site-attached orphan sweep, and the bound site is always returned to the owner's location deck rather than discarded when a company leaves it; **(2) never untaps for the owner** — when the owner re-enters a version of the bound site definition, the destination is placed **tapped** (`mh-hazard-play.ts` step 8), gated by the new shared `siteNeverUntapsForOwner(state, destDef, movingPlayer)` predicate (`reducer-utils.ts`) that recognises both `eddy-lock` and `site-lock` (the `siteEddyLock` never-untap call was replaced by it); **(3) optional `factionInfluenceModifier`** — "-N to each attempt against any faction at any version of this site." `siteFactionInfluenceModifier(state, siteDefId)` (`reducer-utils.ts`) sums the value across bound in-play `site-lock` cards (either player, `!pendingTriggerAttack`) and the site-phase influence path (`legal-actions/site.ts`) adds it to every faction-influence need at that site definition, next to the turn-scoped `influence-at-site-modifier` constraint (a negative value raises the required roll). Used by People Diminished (ba-72): "Playable during the site phase on an untapped Free-hold [{F}] or Border-hold [{B}]. Tap the site. The company faces 3 attacks (Men — 4/8, 3/10, 2/12). Following the attacks, tap a character or discard this card. If this card is not discarded, discard all unique factions playable at the site. -5 to each attempt against any faction at any version of this site. This site is never discarded and never untaps for you. Cannot be duplicated on a given site." — `play-target: site` (`siteType $in [free-hold, border-hold]`) + `play-flag untapped-site-required` + `play-flag tap-site-on-play` + `duplication-limit` scope site + `trigger-attack-on-play` (3× Men, `move-to-mp-pile`, `discardUniqueFactionsAtSite`) + `site-lock` (`factionInfluenceModifier` -5). No character `play-target` (any untapped member may keep). "Balrog specific" is a deck-construction keyword only. Printed MP fixed 0→2 misc (cards.json authoritative). Two optional anti-minion flags extend the same effect for the **hero** counterpart No Strangers at this Time (as-51): `convertDetainmentVsMinion` and `duplicateFirstAutoAttackVsMinion`, both aggregated by `siteLockAntiMinion(state, siteDefId)` (`reducer-utils.ts`, scans both players' bound `!pendingTriggerAttack` `site-lock` cards) and gated by the caller on the defending company's alignment being Ringwraith. `convertDetainmentVsMinion` folds into the `forcesNormalAttacks` gate in `reducer-site.ts` (so a minion company faces the site's detainment auto-attacks as normal — "All detainment attacks at all versions of this site against minion companies instead attack normally"); `duplicateFirstAutoAttackVsMinion` adds one extra combat copying the site's first automatic-attack in the `handleSiteAutomaticAttacks` done-branch, faced exactly once via `SitePhaseState.siteLockMinionAttackDone` ("Against minion companies, each version of this site has an additional automatic-attack: an exact copy … of the first automatic-attack"). As-51 is played through the ordinary hero site-phase permanent-event path (no trigger-attack): `play-target: site` (`siteType $in [free-hold, border-hold]`) + `play-condition: company-context` `company.playedFactionHere` (a new `SitePhaseState.factionPlayedThisSitePhase` flag set by `resolveInfluenceAttemptRoll` on any faction success, exposed via `matchesCompanyContextCondition`) + `duplication-limit` scope site + `site-lock`. The site is already tapped by the required faction play, so no `tap-site-on-play` / `untapped-site-required`. Card data `text`/`effects` were previously stubbed (empty) — filled from cards.json (AS-51).
- `cvcc-attack-permission` — an in-play permanent-event that grants an *extra* Company-vs-Company-combat attack permission beyond the default alignment matrix (`canAttackAlignment`, CoE 8.41). Collected from either player's `cardsInPlay` by `cvccAttackPermitted` (`reducer-utils.ts`) and consulted at all three CvCC-declaration gates that previously only called `canAttackAlignment`: the legal-action generator (`declareCompanyAttackActions`, `legal-actions/site.ts`), the reducer's declare-step transition (`hasCvCCAttackTargets`, `reducer-site.ts`), and the declare-attack validator (`handleDeclareCompanyAttack`, `reducer-site.ts`). The effect's optional `when` matches against `{ attacker: { alignment, isMinion, hasRingwraith }, defender: { alignment, isMinion, hasRingwraith } }` — `isMinion` is true for Ringwraith/Balrog players, `hasRingwraith` is true when any company character has the Ringwraith race (`companyHasRingwraith`). Used by Prone to Violence (ba-42): "Any minion company without a Ringwraith may attack another minion company without a Ringwraith. The attacking company may contain The Balrog." — one `cvcc-attack-permission` (`when` = both companies `isMinion`, both `hasRingwraith: false`; the Balrog attacker is covered by `isMinion`), plus the shipped lifecycle primitives `duplication-limit` (scope game, max 1 — "Cannot be duplicated") and `on-event play-deck-exhausted` → `move` self to discard ("Discard when any play deck is exhausted").

- `site-path-reduction` active constraint — a **player-scoped, turn-scoped** constraint that makes each of the player's moving companies "considered to have one fewer <region type> in its site path" for the rest of the turn. Added via `on-event: self-enters-play` → `add-constraint` (`constraint: "site-path-reduction"`, `target: "player"`, `scope`, and `regionReductions` — a region-type → count map, new branch in `applyShortEventOnEntersPlay`, `reducer-events.ts`). When a moving company's path is resolved (`handleRevealNewSite`, `mh-steps.ts`), the new `applySitePathReduction` helper removes up to that many tokens of each region type from `resolvedSitePath` (and the parallel name entry for region movement; type-only for starter movement), so the reduced path flows uniformly to creature keying (`checkCreatureKeying`), ahunt matching, force-return-to-origin region counts, and end-of-company-MH corruption region counts. Reductions from multiple copies stack (summed per type) and never drop a type below zero. Used by Roam the Waste (ba-73): "Playable during the organization phase if Strangling Coils is in play … Each of your companies this turn is considered to have one fewer Wilderness [{w}] and one fewer Shadow-land [{s}] in its site path." — the `play-window` (organization) + `play-condition` (`card-in-play` "Strangling Coils", attachment-aware since the ba-76 fána is an item on The Balrog) gate + the shared Balrog sideboard-relocation `move` (ba-78 primitive) round out the card. "Balrog specific" is a deck-construction keyword only.
- `character-stat-modifier` with a `requiresCardInPlay` "while <card> in play" gate — the turn-scoped `character-stat-modifier` active constraint (Vilya style; §6a of the DSL) now takes an optional `requiresCardInPlay` card-name field. The effect resolver's `collectCharacterStatModifierEffects` (`engine/effects/resolver.ts`) re-checks it on every effective-stats computation via the attachment-aware `isCardNameInPlayForPlayer` (owner resolved through `findPlayerAndCompany`), so the bonus applies only while the named card remains in play and lapses the instant it leaves — no discard/sweep needed. Stored from `on-event: self-enters-play` → `add-constraint character-stat-modifier` (new field threaded in `reducer-events.ts`). Used by Heart of Dark Fire (ba-63): "Playable if Strangling Coils is in play. … The Balrog receives +5 direct influence this turn **while Strangling Coils is in play**. Cannot be duplicated on a given turn." — `play-window` (organization) + `play-condition` (`card-in-play` "Strangling Coils", attachment-aware since ba-76 is an item on The Balrog) gate the play; `play-target` character (`target.name "The Balrog"`) supplies `targetCharacterId`; the gated `character-stat-modifier` (`direct-influence +5`, scope turn) flows into `effectiveStats.directInfluence` (and thus `availableDI`); `duplication-limit` scope turn max 1 enforces "cannot be duplicated on a given turn"; the shared Balrog sideboard-relocation `move` (ba-78 primitive) surfaces the reshuffle. "Balrog specific" is a deck-construction keyword only.
- `stat-modifier` `target: "company"` **on an attached hazard** + a `when`-gated hazard `grant-action` removal — two generalizations of shipped primitives so a hazard perm-event can penalise a whole company and be removed by a gated activated ability. **(1) Company-target from a hazard** — `collectCompanyItemEffects` (`engine/effects/resolver.ts`) previously scanned only `.items` for `target: "company"` `stat-modifier`/`check-modifier` effects; it now also scans each company member's `.hazards`, and the bearer's own direct hazard pass in `collectCharacterEffects` skips company-target modifiers (mirroring the item pass) so they are collected exactly once per company member — **including the bearer** (contrast `company-others`, which excludes the bearer). Each effect's `when` is evaluated against the **modified** character's context (`bearer.race`, …). **(2) `when`-gated hazard grant-action** — the hazard branch of `grantedActionActivations` (`legal-actions/organization.ts`) now actually enforces a grant-action's `when` gate (it previously logged a failure but still emitted the standard tap-and-roll variant); a failed gate suppresses the standard variant while the corruption no-tap removal variant is still offered. The gate is evaluated against `buildGrantActionContext` (exposing `bearer.atHaven`, `bearer.race`, `site.*`, `phase`, …). Used by Diminish and Depart (ba-17): "Playable on an Elf, Hobbit, or Wizard. All Elves and Hobbits in the target's company have +1 mind, and a Wizard in the company has -1 direct influence. Tap target character at a Haven [{H}] during the organization phase to discard this card. Cannot be duplicated in a given company." — `play-target` character (`target.race $in [elf, hobbit, wizard]`), `duplication-limit` scope company, `stat-modifier` mind +1 `target: company` (`when bearer.race $in [elf, hobbit]`, so every Elf/Hobbit incl. the bearer is boosted and the effective mind flows into the company's general-influence cost), `stat-modifier` direct-influence -1 `target: company` (`when bearer.race: wizard`, flowing into `effectiveStats.directInfluence`/`availableDI`), and a `grant-action remove-self-at-haven` (`cost: { tap: "bearer" }`, `when bearer.atHaven`, `apply` `move` self→discard — the Foolish Words le-112 removal shape without the roll, routed to the hazard owner's discard pile).

- `region-type-remap` (+ `permanent-event-auto-attack` `siteType`) — a long-event environment that reinterprets whole classes of region for creature **keying** and adds an automatic-attack to every site of a printed type. **(1) `siteType` on `permanent-event-auto-attack`** — the augmentation effect (previously targeting only a fixed `siteIds` list of Spawn-type sites) now also accepts a `siteType`, so `collectPermanentEventAttacks` (`engine/manifestations.ts`) appends the attack to every site of that printed type in addition to any listed ids; the Fell Winter attack carries no `onDefeat`/`discardAfterUse`, so it persists (the environment stays in play). **(2) `region-type-remap`** — an environment effect carrying a `remap` table (`{from, to}` region-type pairs) and an optional `when` gate (evaluated against `{ inPlay }`). `collectRegionTypeRemaps(state, inPlayNames)` (`engine/region-keying.ts`) scans both players' `cardsInPlay` for the effect and returns the entries of each card whose gate currently holds (so the remap activates/deactivates live as its gating card enters/leaves play); `applyRegionTypeRemaps(path, remaps)` maps every region of the traversed site path **from its printed type** (all entries applied simultaneously — a `border→wilderness`/`free→border` table never cascades a Free-domain to a Wilderness). Both creature-keying matchers — `findCreatureKeyingMatches` (`legal-actions/movement-hazard.ts`) and `checkCreatureKeying` (`mh-hazard-play.ts`) — apply the remap to the effective region-type path before matching (and before any `region-keying-boost` variants); the underlying path is never mutated. Used by Fell Winter (le-111): "Each Border-hold receives an additional automatic-attack: Wolves — 3 strikes with 7 prowess. Additionally, if Doors of Night is in play, treat all Free-domains as Border-lands and all Border-lands as Wildernesses. Cannot be duplicated." — `permanent-event-auto-attack` (`siteType: "border-hold"`, Wolves 3×7, empty `siteIds`) + `region-type-remap` (`when { inPlay: "Doors of Night" }`, `free→border` + `border→wilderness`) + `duplication-limit` scope game. Also used by Morgul Night (tw-62): "Environment. Playable if Doors of Night is in play. Treat all Wildernesses as Shadow-lands and all Shadow-lands as Dark-domains. Cannot be duplicated." — the same primitive with **no** `when` gate (Doors of Night is a `play-condition` `card-in-play` gate on *playing* the card, not a standing requirement, so the remap survives Doors of Night leaving play) plus `duplication-limit` scope game. Because a remap **replaces** the printed type, Morgul Night also removes `{w}` keying from every Wilderness in the path while it is in play. Also used by Moon (tw-285): "Environment. If Gates of Morning is in play, treat all Wildernesses as Border-lands and all Border-lands as Free-domains for the purposes of playing hazards. If Doors of Night is in play, treat all Dark-domains as Shadow-lands and all Shadow-lands as Wildernesses for the purposes of playing hazards.
