Combined with `playableAt: [{ any: true }]` this is "may play this ally at any site (tapped or untapped) and need not tap himself or the site". **(3) `play-flag: influences-factions`** — the ally "may attempt to influence factions as if he were a character": the faction-influence emitter (`legal-actions/site.ts`) adds untapped flagged company allies to the influencer list (using the ally's printed `directInfluence` field, new on the ally card types), and the declare/resolve reducers (`reducer-site.ts` `handleInfluenceAttemptDeclare` / `resolveInfluenceAttemptRoll`) plus the pending `faction-influence-roll` legal action (`legal-actions/pending.ts`) tap the ally and compute its DI-based need when the `influencingCharacterId` resolves to an ally (via `findAttachment`). **(4) `return-to-hand` effect** (`during: ["organization", "controller-leaves-play"]`) — the org-phase trigger emits a `return-attached-to-hand` action (`legal-actions/organization.ts` `returnAttachedToHandActions`; reducer `reducer-organization.ts` detaches the ally to the owner's hand); the controller-leaves-play trigger routes the ally to the owner's hand instead of the discard pile at every character-leaves-play site via the shared `partitionLeavingAllies` (`reducer-utils.ts`), applied in `discardCharacter` (`pending-reducers.ts`), the combat body-check discard (`combat-actions.ts`), combat elimination (`combat-strike.ts`), and card-discard elimination (`combat-finalize.ts`). **(5) `cancel-strike` `cost: { tap: self }`** on the ally reuses the existing self-tap cancel-strike primitive (no `when`, so it cancels any strike — creature or automatic-attack — directed against the ally, tapping it). Used by Radagast's Black Bird (wh-114).
- `grant-ally-play` — extends ally-play permission from a permanent-event attached to a character (the *bearer*). While in play, any ally matching `filter` (matched as `{ target: allyDef }`, so `target.unique`/`target.mind`/`target.race` are available) becomes playable in the bearer's company at its **current site**, bypassing the ally's printed `playableAt`; with `fromDiscard` it may also be sourced from the **discard pile**; with `excludeBearerControlsCopy` an ally the bearer already controls a copy of (by name) is excluded. `findAllyPlayGrant`/`allyPlayGrantAllowsAlly` (`reducer-utils.ts`) locate the grant among the active company's members' `items` and test a candidate ally. In `legal-actions/site.ts` the hand-ally site-match is relaxed via `grantedByAllyPlay`, and a discard-source loop offers matching discard allies with `fromDiscard: true` (subject to all normal ally gates: untapped site, block-company-joins / no-allies-in-company, an untapped controller, manifestation blocks, company duplication limits, MEWH §10 cross-alignment, Eddy tax). `reducer-site.ts` `handleSitePlayHeroResource` sources a `fromDiscard` play from `discardPile` instead of `hand`. Used by Glove of Radagast (wh-111): a Radagast-specific stage permanent-event (`play-target` character `target.name: "Radagast"` + 2 `stage-points`) — "Any non-unique ally with 1 mind (a copy of which he does not already control) is considered playable with Radagast at his site. This ally may be taken from your discard pile or hand." A player-scoped, Wizardhaven-keyed variant (`atProtectedWizardhavens`) instead lives free-standing in the player's `cardsInPlay` (`findWizardhavenAllyPlayGrant`), extending playability only when the acting company's site is one of the player's own **protected Wizardhavens** (`siteIsProtectedByPlayer` ∧ `isHavenForPlayer`); `allowTappedSite` lifts the untapped-site requirement, and `oncePerSitePhase` limits it to one grant-enabled ally per site phase via a turn-scoped `granted-action-used` lock (`grantedActionUsedThisTurn`, action id `grant-ally-play`) recorded by `handleSitePlayHeroResource` when the play carries `viaWizardhavenAllyGrant`. Only a play that actually depends on the grant (ally not independently playable at the site in its current tap state) consumes the allowance. Used by An Untimely Brood (wh-62): a non-unique Radagast/Alatar stage permanent-event (`stage-points` 4, `duplication-limit` player 1, `play-condition` avatar∈{Radagast,Alatar} ∧ ≥6 stage points ∧ `hasProtectedWizardhaven`) — "One non-unique ally with a mind of 1 is playable at one of your tapped or untapped protected Wizardhavens each of your site phases." A third, simpler variant (`maxCompanySize`) is also player-scoped and free-standing in `cardsInPlay` (`findCompanySizeAllyPlayGrant`), but applies at **any** site (no Wizardhaven restriction) and does not relax *which* allies are playable — it only lifts the untapped-site requirement (`allowTappedSite`) for any company whose `companyEffectiveSize` is at most the given value. Used by Friend of Secret Things (wh-109): a unique Radagast-specific stage permanent-event (`stage-points` 2) — "Your companies with a company size of 2 or less may play allies at tapped sites."
- `site-rule: protected-wizardhaven` + wizard-specific site (deck-construction) — an **inherently protected Wizardhaven**. The site-rule marks a Fallen-wizard haven as always protected for its controller, folded into every protected-site consumer through two shared helpers in `reducer-utils.ts`: `inherentProtectedWizardhavenOwner` (the Fallen-wizard for whom the site is a Wizardhaven — `isHavenForPlayer` — and who counts as the avatar named by the site's `<wizard>-specific` keyword, if any) and `isSiteProtectedForPlayer(state, siteDefId, playerId, match)`, which ORs the inherent case onto the existing `site-protected` constraint check (`match: 'self'` = protected by that player; `'opponent'` = protected by someone else). All prior constraint reads now route through it — `isProtectedWizardhavenFor` (Deep Mines wh-55 descent source, `legal-actions/organization-companies.ts`), `siteIsProtectedByPlayer` / `siteIsProtectedAgainstPlayer` (`legal-actions/site.ts`, the "at your protected Wizardhaven" faction/event play-conditions and the opponent marshalling-point block), and the `site-protected` play-condition checks in `legal-actions/site.ts` / `organization-events.ts` — plus `playerHasProtectedWizardhaven` (the `player.hasProtectedWizardhaven` flag for A Strident Spawn wh-61 / An Untimely Brood wh-62), which additionally returns true while one of the player's companies occupies an inherently protected Wizardhaven. The "Only Radagast's companies may use this card" restriction is the existing `radagast-specific` keyword: deck-construction rule 1.07 (`deck-validation.ts`) now also scans the `sites` section, so the site can only be included when the matching avatar is declared. Used by Rhosgobel (wh-57): "Only Radagast's companies may use this card. This site is a protected Wizardhaven [{H}]. … all attacks against it are canceled" (`cancel-attacks`) "… You receive the stage point if any of your companies are at this site" (`stage-points` `whileCompanyAtSite`).

- `prohibit-company-events` — a game-wide hazard permanent-event effect that suppresses **resource permanent-events played on a company as a whole** (Fellowship tw-240: `play-target: company` → `CardInPlay.companyId`) for every company containing a character of `companyHasRace`. Two faces share `collectProhibitedCompanyEventRaces` (`reducer-utils.ts`, scans both players' `cardsInPlay`, skips `pendingTriggerAttack`): **(1) Discard** — `sweepProhibitedCompanyEvents` (a `postReduce` sweep in `reducer.ts`, via the shared `discardCardsInPlayWhere`) discards every `companyId`-bound resource permanent-event (cardType `hero-resource-event`/`minion-resource-event`, `eventType: "permanent"`) whose bound company contains a prohibited race to its owner's discard pile, clearing its `activeConstraints`; running continuously it also catches a matching character joining a company that already carries such an event. **(2) Prohibition** — `isCompanyEventPlayProhibited` blocks the organization-phase `play-target: company` emitter (`legal-actions/organization-events.ts`) from offering such a card on a matching company. Character-attached permanent-events (which set `attachedTo`, not `companyId`) are untouched ("not individual characters"). Used by Stormcrow (td-73): "Discard all resource permanent-events that have been played on each company with a Wizard … No such cards may be played on each Wizard's company." — `prohibit-company-events` (`companyHasRace: "wizard"`), paired with two `all-characters` `direct-influence` `stat-modifier`s (base -2 gated `target.race: wizard`, an additional -2 gated `$and[target.race: wizard, inPlay: "Doors of Night"]` = net -4 with DoN — the ba-25 base+softening shape, but for direct influence, which flows through `computeEffectiveStats` into `effectiveStats.directInfluence`/`availableDI` for every Wizard on either side), `on-event play-deck-exhausted` → self-discard `move` (the No Way Forward dm-75 / Rumor of the One le-224 shape, fired by `completeDeckExhaust`), and `duplication-limit` scope `game` max 1.

- Gandalf's Wizardhaven-conversion (reuse of Hidden Haven wh-75) + `"Any <site-type>"` home-site form — Chambers in the Royal Court (wh-97) converts a **hero Free-hold** into Gandalf's Wizardhaven and, unlike Hidden Haven (a fallen-wizard hero-resource played during the site phase), is a `gandalf-specific` **Stage** resource played during the organization phase (rule 5.F1, the Guarded Haven wh-74 site-target path in `playPermanentEventActions`). It reuses the five Hidden-Haven `on-event self-enters-play → add-constraint` effects verbatim — `site-type-override` (`overrideType: haven`), `wizardhaven-conversion` (player-scoped, so "Other Fallen-wizards may not use this site as a Wizardhaven" = the conversion never applies to an opponent via `isHavenForPlayer`/`isWizardhavenConversionFor`), `skip-automatic-attacks`, `site-nothing-playable` (→ `site-nothing-playable-as-written`), `cancel-attacks-at-site` — plus a `play-target` site filter `{$and:[{cardType: hero-site},{siteType: free-hold}]}` ("your hero Free-hold [{F}]", matched on the *printed* type in the org-phase site-target branch) and `duplication-limit` scope `site` ("Cannot be duplicated on a given site"). All five constraints are `until-cleared`, sourced from the card and discarded by `discardOrphanedSiteAttachedEvents` once no company occupies the bound site (= "discarded when the site is discarded or returned to its location deck; cannot be discarded otherwise"). **New engine work:** "and is one of Gandalf's home sites" — `homesiteMatchesSite` (`legal-actions/organization-characters.ts`) now resolves the `"Any <site-type>"` home-site form (Gandalf wh-4 prints `"Any Free-hold"`) via a `SITE_TYPE_HOMESITE_LABELS` map, matching the site's **printed** `siteType` (Dark-hold excluded, keeping its bespoke Balrog-remap/Darkhaven handling). Because it reads the printed type, a Free-hold converted by wh-97 stays one of Gandalf's home sites even though its effective type now reads `haven` — the operative clause, since a Fallen-wizard avatar is home-site-only (generic havens excluded) and so relies on the home-site match to be (re-)brought into play there.

- `grant-creature-keying` region-type branch + `requiresNonCoastalKeying` — extends the ba-28 (Ungoliant's Foul Issue) in-play creature-keying grant so an environment may open **region-type** keying, not just site-type. `siteFilter.regionTypes` adds a branch OR'd with the existing site-type/keyword branch: `inPlayGrantsCreatureKeying` (`legal-actions/movement-hazard.ts`, now passed `mhState`) grants the keying when the moving company's `resolvedSitePath` contains one of those region types (the site-type branch still checks the effective destination/current site type AND its keywords). The optional top-level `requiresNonCoastalKeying` gates the grant on the creature itself: it applies only to creatures whose own printed `keyedTo` offers at least one non-Coastal-Sea region (`creatureHasNonCoastalRegionKeying` — any `regionNames`, or any `regionTypes` other than `coastal`/`coastal-sea`), so a Coastal-Sea-only creature (Fell Turtle tw-34) is excluded. Both feed the same `keyedBy: { method: "keying-bypass" }` path (the reducer trusts the offer and skips re-keying). Used by A Pack at the Door (tw-497): a **long-event** gated `play-condition` `card-in-play` "Doors of Night" — "Each non-unique Animal, Spider and Wolf creature may be played in Border-lands [{b}], Border-holds [{B}] or Ruins & Lairs [{R}]. The creature must be playable in a non-Coastal Sea [{c}] region." — `grant-creature-keying` (`creatureFilter` `$and` race `$in` animal/spider/wolf + `unique $ne true`, `siteFilter` `regionTypes: [border]` + `siteTypes: [border-hold, ruins-and-lairs]`, `requiresNonCoastalKeying: true`).

- Site-phase Wizardhaven-conversion gated on stage points (reuse of Hidden Haven wh-75 + Delver's Harvest wh-65 timing) — Mischief in a Mean Way (wh-77) converts a **Border-hold** into one of the Fallen-wizard's Wizardhavens during the **site phase** with no new engine work, combining three existing primitives. It is a `stage`-aligned `minion-resource-event`, so rule 5.F1 would otherwise restrict it to the organization phase; the Delver's Harvest (wh-65) exception admits it into the site phase because it carries a `play-condition` `requires: active-company` (the stage-resource site-phase gate in `legal-actions/site.ts` lines ~872–886), here with `condition: { "site.type": "border-hold" }` (the printed type from `buildActiveCompanyContext`). A `play-condition` `requires: player-state` `{ "player.stagePoints": { "$gte": 10 } }` enforces "if you have 10 or more stage points" (the Gatherer of Loyalties wh-70 stage-point gate, evaluated via `buildPlayerStateContext`). A `play-target` site `{ siteType: "border-hold" }` binds the card to the company's current Border-hold (embeds `targetSiteDefinitionId` in the play action so the conversion attaches to that site — the wh-75/wh-97 site-binding path). It then reuses the five Hidden-Haven `on-event self-enters-play → add-constraint` effects verbatim (`site-type-override` → haven, player-scoped `wizardhaven-conversion` = "Other Fallen-wizards may not use this site", `skip-automatic-attacks`, `site-nothing-playable` → `site-nothing-playable-as-written`, `cancel-attacks-at-site`), all `until-cleared` and swept by `discardOrphanedSiteAttachedEvents` when no company occupies the bound site (= "discard when the site is discarded / returned to its location deck; cannot be discarded otherwise"), plus a `stage-points` (2) contribution. Card-test-only shared-helper change: `buildFallenWizardSitePhaseState` gained an optional `stagePoints` param (set after the initial recompute).

- `play-condition player-state` context — `characterCount` + `protectedWizardhavenCount`. Two additive fields on `buildPlayerStateContext` (`legal-actions/organization.ts`): `player.characterCount` = the number of characters the player controls in play; `player.protectedWizardhavenCount` = the number of **distinct** protected Wizardhaven sites the player controls (`protectedWizardhavenCount` in `reducer-utils.ts`, the counting sibling of the boolean `playerHasProtectedWizardhaven` — same predicate, deduped by site `CardDefinitionId`). These extend the existing player-state gate (`stagePoints`, `factionCount`, `hasProtectedWizardhaven`, `avatar`, `inPlay`) for cards needing *counts* of characters / protected Wizardhavens. Await the Onset (wh-96): "Playable if you are Gandalf and have at least: 12 stage points, two protected Wizardhavens [{H}], 4 factions, and 6 characters." → `play-condition player-state` `$and[stagePoints ≥ 12, protectedWizardhavenCount ≥ 2, factionCount ≥ 4, characterCount ≥ 6]` (the Gandalf gate is the `gandalf-specific` keyword), plus `duplication-limit` scope `game`. Await the Onset (wh-96) is now **certified** — its two marshalling-point clauses are implemented as pin-to-1 overrides (`played-after-faction-mp-pin` and `nonhaven-company-mp-pin`, below); a Fallen-wizard never stores factions at a site, so neither needs a faction-location model.

- `played-after-faction-mp-pin` — stamps every faction the controller **influences into play after this card is in play** with a per-instance MP pin (`CardInPlay.mpPinned = value`), set in the faction-influence reducer (`reducer-site.ts`, via `playedAfterFactionMpPin`). A stamped faction scores exactly `value` at the top of the `cardsInPlay` loop in `recompute-derived.ts` (`addPinnedCardMp`), ahead of `noncharacter-mp-override` / `faction-mp-override` / the §4 clamp — "regardless of other cards in play". Factions played *before* keep their normal value (the "place these factions under Await the Onset" bookkeeping). Used by Await the Onset (wh-96) clause A.

- `nonhaven-company-mp-pin` — pins every MP-scoring card **held by a company not at one of the controller's Wizardhavens** to a flat `value` MP, overriding all other MP computation ("regardless of other cards in play"): the §4 clamp, `fw-character-ally-mp` (Great Patron wh-72), the `*-mp-full` exemptions, and `noncharacter-mp-override` (Give Welcome wh-99). Applies to characters and the items/allies they bear — never to factions or other `cardsInPlay` entries, which are not "in a company" (a Fallen-wizard never stores factions at a site). Wizardhaven membership is read from `Company.currentSite` via `isHavenForPlayer` (his own Wizardhaven sites + any Hidden-Haven conversion); every other site — and a company with no current site — counts as outside. Collected per player by `nonHavenCompanyMpPin` and applied in the company-scoring loop of `recompute-derived.ts` (`addPinnedCardMp`), reusing the same per-company-site machinery as the Under-deeps callable-MP mirror. The card's "when the game ends" qualifier is modelled as a continuous override (no separate end-of-game scoring pass); for a Fallen-wizard this is normally a no-op (the §4 clamp already values each company-held card at 1), so it only changes a total when another effect has boosted a card above the pin value. Used by Await the Onset (wh-96) clause B.

- `protect-from-removal` + `character-removal-protected` constraint (Tookish Blood tw-104 resource mode) — a dual-mode `hazard-event` short that is also `playable-as-resource`. The **hazard mode** reuses the certified `call-of-home-check` primitive (a `play-target` character `filter { target.race: hobbit }` + `threshold: 11`): roll + the Hobbit-owner's unused GI < 11 returns the Hobbit to hand, exactly as Call of Home (tw-18 / le-105). The **resource mode** is a new `protect-from-removal` effect: `playShortEventActions` (`legal-actions/organization-events.ts`) offers a `play-short-event` on each of the controller's own characters matching the companion `play-target` filter, and `handlePlayShortEvent` (`reducer-events.ts`) installs a turn-scoped `character-removal-protected` `ActiveConstraint` on the target (`engine/removal-protection.ts::addRemovalProtection`) and discards the card. While the constraint is present, the two central character-removal helpers in `pending-reducers.ts` — `returnCharacterToHand` (dice-check `return-character-to-hand` branch, incl. this card's own hazard mode) and the `'discard'` path of `discardCharacter` — fizzle via `isCharacterRemovalProtected`; an elimination to out-of-play is untouched. The `{ kind: 'turn' }` scope auto-clears at turn end (`pending.ts` `turn-end` sweep). Note: the M/H hazard-play env-cancel gate (`movement-hazard.ts`) previously hijacked **any** `playable-as-resource` hazard as an environment-canceler (Twilight); it now excludes character-targeting resource hazards (`call-of-home-check` / `protect-from-removal`) so Tookish Blood's hazard mode reaches the character-target branch.

- `call-of-home-check` gains `rollModifiers` + item transfer (Call of the Sea tw-19) — two additions to the shared primitive, both retroactively benefiting every card that uses it (tw-18, le-105, tw-104). (1) An optional `rollModifiers: { when, value }[]` list, evaluated in `chain-reducer.ts` at dice-check enqueue time against `{ company: { sitePathRegionTypes: RegionType[] } }` (read directly off `MovementHazardPhaseState.resolvedSitePath` — the target always belongs to the company currently in its M/H sub-phase); matching entries sum into a `constant` `DiceCheckModifier` alongside the existing `unused-gi` one, mirroring `company-tap-roll`'s per-character `rollModifiers` pattern (§44b). Used for "modified by -3 if the character's company moved this turn using a site path containing a Coastal Sea [{c}]" — `{ "when": { "company.sitePathRegionTypes": { "$includes": "coastal" } }, "value": -3 }`. (2) The dice-check's `onFail: { type: "return-character-to-hand" }` branch now sets `allowItemTransfer: true`, reusing the Pilfer Anything Unwatched (as-33) `transfer-returned-item` pending resolution so "any one item held by the removed character may automatically be transferred to another character in his company" actually offers the transfer instead of unconditionally discarding every item — this was previously unwired on tw-18/le-105/tw-104 despite their identical printed text. Card: "Playable on a Elf character. The character's player makes a roll. Return the character to the player's hand if this result plus his unused general influence is less than 10. This result is modified by -3 if the character's company moved this turn using a site path containing a Coastal Sea [{c}]. Any one item held by a character removed in this fashion may automatically be transferred to another character in his company (all other non-follower cards target character controls are discarded)." — `play-target` character (`filter: { target.race: "elf" }`) + `call-of-home-check` (`threshold: 10`, the `rollModifiers` entry above).

- `move` `from: hazards-on-target` — a new `MoveZone` (`types/effects.ts`, collector in `reducer-move.ts`) that locates every hazard permanent-event attached to the played short-event's target character (`ctx.targetCharacterId`), applies the move `filter`, and routes each to its **owner's** discard (owner resolved from the instance-id prefix `<playerId>-<counter>`, falling back to the holder's opponent — hazards on a hero character are played by the opposing hazard player). Mirrors `items-on-target`/`allies-on-target` but reads `char.hazards` and does prefix-based ownership like the `attached-to-character` locator. Distinct from the Wizard Uncloaked (td-169) bounce, which returns a whole *company*'s hazards to the *opponent's hand*; this removes a single character's hazards to their owners' discard. Driven from `handlePlayResourceShortEvent` (`reducer-events.ts`) via `applyMove` when a played short-event carries a `move { select: filter-all, from: hazards-on-target }`. Used by The Sun Unveiled (as-56): "Remove all hazard permanent-events on the character and, if tapped, untap him." — playability (`play-target` character filtered on `company.siteType: free-hold` + `inPlay: "Gates of Morning"`, both from `buildPlayOptionContext`), removal (this move, filter permanent `hazard-event`), and the untap (`on-event self-enters-play → set-character-status untapped`, idempotent) are all existing/DSL primitives; the FW-deck ban is enforced by `FALLEN_WIZARD_BANNED_CARD_IDS` in `deck-validation.ts`.

- `check-modifier` `target: "all-in-play"` (game-wide influence/offering penalty from a hazard long-event) — a third scope for the `check-modifier` family, alongside the bearer default, `company`, and `player-in-play`. Where `player-in-play` (ba-38) is collected only from the **influencing** player's own bare `cardsInPlay` and thus benefits only its owner, an `all-in-play` modifier is summed by `collectGlobalCheckModifier` (`reducer-utils.ts`) — a scan of **both** players' `cardsInPlay` for bare, unattached in-play events (permanent- or long-event), mirroring `collectFactionInfluenceRestriction` — so it applies to **every** matching check by **either** player, gated by the effect's `when` against the check resolver context. Folded into the faction-influence need at all three influence sites: the display generator (`legal-actions/site.ts`), the paused faction-influence-roll display (`legal-actions/pending.ts`), and the roll resolver (`reducer-site.ts` `resolveInfluenceAttemptRoll`). The card carries both `influence` and `offering` check kinds (the METD offering attempt has no distinct engine resolution flow yet, same as Foolish Words td-25; the influence penalty is the concrete, exercised mechanic). A hazard long-event resolves bare into the declaring player's `cardsInPlay` via `in-play-general` (`chain-reducer.ts` `resolveLongEvent`), where the both-players scan finds it. Used by Times Are Evil (td-76): "All offering attempts and influence attempts are modified by -3." Also used unchanged by Scatha at Home (td-62): "-1 to all influence attempts." — a bare `check: "influence"` (no `offering`), `value: -1`, carried by a **permanent**-event rather than a long-event (the both-players `cardsInPlay` scan admits either), alongside its `dragon-at-home` Gondmaeglom augmentation; no new engine work needed.

- `faction-mp-bonus` + `discard-on-card-leaves-play` (Alliance of Free Peoples as-45) — two new primitives on one hero `permanent-event`. **`faction-mp-bonus`** grants an *additional* marshalling point to a whole class of the controller's in-play factions while a race-diversity **gate** holds: `requireEachRace` lists the races that must *each* be present (≥1 in-play faction per race) for the bonus to apply, and `races` lists which controlled factions receive `bonus` MP. Collected by `factionMpBonusEntries` and applied in a dedicated additive faction-MP pass in `recompute-derived.ts` (after the leader-control group bonus), scanning only the controller's own non-set-aside `cardsInPlay` factions — a *separate* pass so the +1 still lands on factions whose base MP was resolved by an override branch that `continue`d. The two race lists are independent (a card may gate on one set, boost another). "Man" is the literal faction race, so a Dúnadan faction is neither counted toward the gate nor boosted. **`discard-on-card-leaves-play`** discards the carrier the moment another card matching its `filter` (matched as `{ card: def }`) leaves the controller's play area — evaluated as a `postReduce` prev/next diff (`engine/discard-on-card-leaves.ts::applyDiscardOnCardLeaves`, wired into `reducer.ts` beside `applyEvilHourTaps`), the same reactive-diff pattern as A More Evil Hour. It fires on the *event* of a matching card disappearing from `cardsInPlay` regardless of how it left, and even when a race-diversity gate would still hold afterwards (losing one of two Man factions) — matching the printed "any … faction … discarded" wording, distinct from the single-state `discard-self-when` (Prophet of Doom wh-106). Used by Alliance of Free Peoples (as-45): "If at least one hero Dwarf faction, one hero Elf faction, and one hero Man faction is in play, all hero Dwarf factions, hero Elf factions, and hero Man factions give an additional marshalling point. Discard when any hero Dwarf faction, hero Elf faction, or hero Man faction is discarded from play. Cannot be duplicated." — `faction-mp-bonus` (`requireEachRace`/`races` = dwarf/elf/man, bonus 1) + `discard-on-card-leaves-play` (filter `$and`[`card.cardType` = hero-resource-faction, `card.race` `$in` dwarf/elf/man]) + the existing `duplication-limit` scope `game` ("Cannot be duplicated", checked in `organization-events.ts`).

- `agent-reveal-site-override` + `fetch-agent-to-hand` (the "Inner Cunning family" of dual permanent-/short-event agent hazards) — two data-driven primitives keyed by a `homeSiteTypes: SiteType[]` list, established by Inner Cunning (dm-68, Shadow-hold/Dark-hold) and reused verbatim by Nobody's Friend (dm-76, Border-hold/Free-hold). **Mode 1 (`agent-reveal-site-override`)**: a hazard permanent-event played on one of the hazard player's own **face-down agents brought into play this turn** (`!revealed && !inPlayAtTurnStart`; one `play-hazard` action with `altEventMode: 'permanent-event'` + `targetAgentId` per eligible agent, emitted in `legal-actions/movement-hazard.ts`). It resolves into `cardsInPlay` bound via `CardInPlay.attachedToAgentId` (`chain-reducer.ts`). While attached — and only if the agent's printed home site is of one of the override types (`agentHomeSiteMatchesTypes` in `reducer-utils.ts`, classifying each home-site name against the pool preferring the agent's own alignment) — `revealAgentActions` broadens the reveal-site choice from the printed home-site names to ANY location-deck site of those types. On reveal the orphaned-agent-attached-event sweep (`reducer-utils.ts`) discards the card ("Discard when the agent is revealed"). **Mode 2 (`fetch-agent-to-hand`)**: a hazard short-event (`altEventMode: 'short-event'`, viable only when a matching agent is in the play deck) that on chain resolution enqueues a `fetch-to-deck` pending effect (source play deck, `to: 'hand'`, shuffle, revealToOpponent) whose `homeSiteTypes` restriction is enforced in the `fetch-from-pile` reducer. **Both modes** are blocked when the opponent is a minion player (`isMinionOrBalrog`).

- `check-modifier` `onFailure: "shuffle-faction-into-deck"` + `target.isInfluencing` + `player.playsAsSauron` (The Dark Power as-79) — three small extensions to the one-shot influence-boost subsystem. (1) The `add-constraint check-modifier` apply may carry `onFailure: "shuffle-faction-into-deck"` (threaded through `chain-reducer.ts` onto `constraint.kind.onFailure`); when the consuming faction-influence roll **fails**, `resolveInfluenceAttemptRoll` (`reducer-site.ts`) shuffles the faction into its player's play deck (via `shuffle` from `rng.js`, advancing the RNG) instead of discarding it. Success is unchanged. (2) `target.isInfluencing` in `buildPlayOptionContext` (`legal-actions/organization.ts`) is `true` only for the character whose `influence-attempt` entry is live (unresolved, un-negated) in the chain — a booster that modifies "an influence check" without naming a target pins its `play-target` to the influencing character, so the one-shot constraint lands on and is consumed by exactly the boosted check. (3) `player.playsAsSauron` in `buildPlayerStateContext` exposes `playerPlaysAsSauron` (a `play-as-sauron` marker in play — The Lidless Eye le-203 / Sauron ba-43) to `play-condition` `requires: "player-state"`. Used by The Dark Power (as-79): "Playable if you are Sauron. +3 to an influence check against a faction. If the check is not successful, shuffle the faction into your play deck." — `play-condition` player-state (`player.playsAsSauron`) + `play-target` character (`target.isInfluencing`) + `play-option` add-constraint check-modifier influence +3, scope `until-cleared`, `onFailure: "shuffle-faction-into-deck"` (the Muster shape).

- `grant-action` `discard-minors-for-gold-ring` + `gold-ring-item-unlocked` constraint (Hermit's Hill le-382) — the minion sibling of the hero Hermit's Hill (dm-32) `discard-minors-for-major` primitive. A site-phase grant-action with no character actor: `sitePhaseGrantActions` (`legal-actions/site.ts`) offers one `activate-granted-action` per unordered pair of minor items borne by the active company (`targetCardId`/`secondTargetCardId`), only while the site is untapped and — unlike the hero version — only for a **covert** company (`isCovertCompany`). The shared reducer handler (`handleDiscardMinorsForUnlock`, `reducer-site.ts`) discards both minors and adds a `gold-ring-item-unlocked` constraint scoped `company-site-phase`. While it binds the company, a gold-ring item is playable at the site "regardless of its text restrictions": `playResourcesActions` bypasses both the site's `playableResources` gate and the ring's own `item-play-site` restriction (the Saruman's-Machinery bypass pattern), without bypassing the site-tap gate — playing the ring taps the site (CRF: the site must be untapped), naturally limiting the unlock to one ring. Used by Hermit's Hill (le-382): "During the site phase, a covert company may discard two minor items they bear to make any one gold ring item (regardless of its text restrictions) playable at this untapped site this turn."

- `cancel-attack` from-hand + `play-condition` `player-state` gate (Eye Never Sleeping as-82) — the from-hand cancel-attack paths in `cancelAttackActions` (`legal-actions/combat.ts`) now evaluate a `play-condition` `requires: "player-state"` on the card (both the generic costless/character-gated loop and the covert dual-faction hand mode), against the same `buildPlayerStateContext` used by the organization-phase and any-phase short-event paths — so a "Playable if …" gate on the player's own state holds on the combat play path too. `player.playsAsSauron` is exposed in `buildPlayerStateContext` (also added independently by The Dark Power as-79): true while the player counts as Sauron via a `play-as-sauron` marker in play (The Lidless Eye le-203 / Sauron ba-43). Additionally, both combat-only short-event classifiers (`heroResourceShortEventActions` in `long-event.ts` and `playResourceShortEventActions` in `organization.ts`) now treat `play-condition` as a neutral companion effect — a gated cancel-attack card stays combat-only and is never offered as a do-nothing generic short event outside combat. Used by Eye Never Sleeping (as-82): "Playable if you are Sauron. Cancel one hazard creature attack." — `play-condition` player-state (`player.playsAsSauron`) + a costless `cancel-attack` with `when` `attack.source $in ["creature", "on-guard-creature"]` (the le-153 "hazard creature attack" gate — automatic attacks and CvCC are excluded), routed through the chain via the Dark Quarrels (tw-207) from-hand path.

- `on-event: site-revealed-as-new-site` → `tap-one-character` (Himring as-150) — a site-printed on-event fired the moment a moving company's destination site is revealed and its path declared (`fireSiteRevealedAsNewSite` in `mh-steps.ts`, called from `handleRevealNewSite`'s declare-path branch for every movement type). The effect's `when` is evaluated against `{ company: { covert } }` ({@link isCovertCompany}), so "an overt company" is `{ "company.covert": false }` and the covert-company variant (Isles of the Dead That Live as-154) uses `true`. A matching `tap-one-character` apply enqueues the existing tap-one-character pending resolution (Stench of Mordor le-141) scoped `company-mh-subphase`, blocking the company's M/H flow until the resource player taps a character. `tapOneCharacterActions` (`legal-actions/pending.ts`) was tightened for the shared "must tap one untapped character if available" wording: `pass` is now emitted **only** when the company has no untapped character (previously always) — this also corrects Stench of Mordor. Himring's remaining rules reuse shipped primitives: the Undead auto-attack's "each character wounded must make a corruption check modified by -2" is the `on-event: character-wounded-by-self` → `force-check` machinery (Barrow-downs le-353), and structural data (sitePath {w}{c}{c}, minor/major items, 3/3 draws) is engine-standard.

- `on-event: site-revealed-as-new-site` reuse (Isle of the Ulond as-153) — pure reuse of the Himring (as-150) primitive with the same overt gate `{ "company.covert": false }` → `tap-one-character`. Everything else on the card is structural: Dragon auto-attack (1 strike, 14 prowess), Information/minor/major playability, sitePath {w}{w}{c} from Geann a-Lisch, 2/2 draws.

- `on-event: site-revealed-as-new-site` + `site-rule: deny-item` reuse (Tolfalas as-162) — pure reuse; the minion twin of the hero Tolfalas (tw-433) that also carries Himring's (as-150) reveal-tap. Three shipped primitives combined with no engine change: `on-event: character-wounded-by-self` → `force-check` corruption (modifier -2) for "each character wounded must make a corruption check modified by -2" (Barrow-downs le-353, Himring as-150); `site-rule: deny-item` with `{ "subtype": "greater", "name": { "$ne": "Scroll of Isildur" } }` for "greater* — Scroll of Isildur only" (tw-433); and `on-event: site-revealed-as-new-site` gated `{ "company.covert": false }` → `tap-one-character` for the overt reveal-tap (Himring as-150). Everything else is structural: Undead auto-attack (3 strikes, 7 prowess), minor/major/greater playability, sitePath {s}{w}{w}{c} from Minas Morgul, 2/2 draws.

- `site-rule: cancel-auto-attacks` with `"scope": "printed"` (Rhosgobel as-159) — removes ALL of a site's *printed* automatic-attacks while the rule's generic `when` condition holds, evaluated against the card-name context `{ inPlayAnywhere, charactersInPlayAnywhere }` (`cancelAutoAttacksConditionMet` in `manifestations.ts`). Rhosgobel declares `{ "charactersInPlayAnywhere": { "$includes": "Radagast" } }` — matched by name, not definition ID, so every version of the card counts (Radagast is tw-178 hero Wizard and wh-8 Fallen-wizard). Applied in `getActiveAutoAttacks()` before hazard augments, so attacks added by Spawn permanent-events / `extra-automatic-attack` constraints survive. The `"scope": "first"` variant of the same rule (The Under-gates dm-38 / as-165) instead slices the first attack off the final combined list, on `{ "inPlayAnywhere": { "$includes": "Balrog of Moria" } }`. Used by Rhosgobel (as-159): "If the Wizard card Radagast is in play, the automatic-attacks are removed." — rest of the card is structural (two Maia 1×13 auto-attacks, Information/minor playability, {d} path from Dol Guldur, 1/1 draws).

- `site-rule: deny-company-move` + `site-rule: deny-company-attack` (Rivendell as-160, the minion Elf-haven) — two condition-gated site prohibitions. **`deny-company-move`** ("A Ringwraith may not move to this site") bars a company matching `when` (context `{ company: { hasRingwraith } }` via `companyHasRingwraith` — RW avatar or follower) from declaring movement to the site: `siteDeniesCompanyMove` (`reducer-utils.ts`) filters the per-company candidate list in `planMovementActions` (`legal-actions/organization-companies.ts`, before every pass — regular/starter, Eagle/Gwaihir, Under-deeps, Deep Mines) with a `handlePlanMovement` reducer backstop. **`deny-company-attack`** ("A minion company may not attack another company at this site") suppresses CvCC at the location: `siteDeniesCompanyAttack` (`reducer-utils.ts`) matches `when` against the shared CvCC context (`buildCvCCContext`, same shape as `cvcc-attack-permission`: `{ attacker/defender: { alignment, isMinion, hasRingwraith } }`) on **both** companies' current site cards (hero/minion twins share the location by name) and beats any `cvcc-attack-permission` grant; checked in `declareCompanyAttackActions` (`legal-actions/site.ts`), `opponentHasAttackableCompanyAtSite` and `handleDeclareCompanyAttack` (`reducer-site.ts`). The card's third rule ("Any company moving to this site has its hazard limit increased by 2") reuses `site-rule: hazard-limit-modifier` (Barad-dûr tw-374). Edhellond as-145 reuses this exact triple (the three site-rules, identical `when` clauses). Siblings with identical text still open: Grey Havens as-149, Lórien as-155.

- `site-rule: ringwraith-reanimate-from-discard` (Urlurtsu Nurn le-409, the minion Dark-hold) — an **org-phase site ability that brings a character from the discard pile into play as a new company**, tapping the player's Ringwraith at the site as the cost, plus a turn-scoped "move-away-or-be-discarded" movement constraint. The rule carries a DSL `filter` matched against the discard-pile character definition. A dedicated org-phase emitter `siteRingwraithReanimateActivations` (`legal-actions/organization.ts`, wired into `organizationActions` beside `siteSageRingTestActivations`) scans each company whose `currentSite` carries the rule, finds an untapped avatar of race `ringwraith` present in that company, and emits one `reanimate-from-discard` action per eligible discard-pile character (skipping uniques already in play and manifestations of an in-play entity, mirroring `recruit-via-event`). The reducer `handleReanimateFromDiscard` (`reducer-organization.ts`, registered in `ORGANIZATION_HANDLERS`) re-validates the Ringwraith/site/filter, taps the Ringwraith, removes the character from the discard pile, and mints it under general influence into a new `Company` sharing the in-play site instance (`siteCardOwned: false`, the sibling-at-occupied-site pattern) tagged with a new `reanimatedRingwraithId` company field; the play is free of the one-character-per-turn slot (the tap is the cost). The constraint is enforced at the M/H→Site boundary by `discardStrandedReanimatedCompanies` (`mh-hazard-play.ts`, called from `finalizeCompanyMH`'s all-companies-handled branch, before `autoMergeNonHavenCompanies`): a reanimated company still sharing a site (compared by definition id) with its Ringwraith's current company has its character(s) discarded to the owner's discard pile via `discardCharacterToDiscardPile` (a new exported thin wrapper over the `discardCharacter` helper in `pending-reducers.ts`); one that reached a different site keeps its character(s) and has the turn-scoped marker cleared. `format-actions.ts` gained a `reanimate-from-discard` case. Used by Urlurtsu Nurn (le-409): "If your Ringwraith is at this site, he may tap during the organization phase to bring one Orc or Troll character from your discard pile into play at this site (as another company). The character must move to a different site from that of your Ringwraith this turn or be discarded at the end of the movement/hazard phase." (Its hero twin dm-42 has no special ability.)

- Minion Under-deeps site, pure structural reuse (The Iron-deeps as-152) — the first Ringwraith-aligned Under-deeps site certified, proving the Under-deeps machinery (`under-deeps` keyword, `adjacentSites`, `getUnderDeepsReachable`/`getUnderDeepsRequiredRoll`) is alignment-agnostic. Data fix pass: the imported entry was missing the `under-deeps` keyword, the `adjacentSites` map (Carn Dûm 0 / The Under-leas 5 / The Under-vaults 6 — minion rolls differ from the Balrog twin ba-91's 6/7), and the printed "(detainment)" marker on the Trolls 3x10 auto-attack, encoded as an unfiltered site-level `combat-detainment` (as-139/as-141/ba-102 precedent; unconditional for any defender, unlike the §3.II.2.R1 alignment branch). Adjacency names resolve per-alignment (minion Carn Dûm is the Darkhaven le-359; The Under-leas/The Under-vaults are as-167/as-168). Same pure-reuse pass applied to The Sulfur-deeps (as-161, Dol Guldur le-367 / The Under-courts 4 / The Pûkel-deeps 9 / The Under-gates 4 / The Under-galleries 7; Trolls 2x10 detainment) and The Under-courts (as-163, Barad-dûr le-352 / The Sulfur-deeps 4 / The Under-galleries 4; Trolls 3x10 detainment) — both were missing `adjacentSites`, `combat-detainment`, and needed no new engine work. Check the remaining AS under-deeps siblings (as-164..168) for the same missing-adjacentSites import bug when certifying them.

- Minion Under-deeps site, conditional-detainment + non-Nazgûl override combined (The Under-leas as-167) — the Ringwraith twin of ba-102, reusing only shipped primitives. Its "(detainment against **overt** company)" 1st automatic-attack is the *overt mirror* of Minas Tirith le-391's covert-gated attack: a site-level `combat-detainment` with `when: { "defender.covert": false }` (the matcher treats a literal `false` as equality, so it fires for an overt/non-covert defender and skips a covert Fallen-wizard company; against a Ringwraith company §3.II.2.R1 independently forces detainment since the site is a Shadow-hold). Its Special "Non-Nazgûl creatures played at this site attack normally, not as detainment" is `site-rule: attacks-not-detainment` (Moria le-392 / The Under-gates as-165 precedent) but with a **compound** filter `{ "attack.automatic": false, "enemy.race": { "$ne": "ringwraith" } }` — the `attack.automatic: false` clause (ba-102) protects the 1st automatic-attack's own `combat-detainment` while `enemy.race != nazgul` restricts the normal-attack override to non-Nazgûl creatures; both clauses AND. No engine change — all three effects (`dynamic-auto-attack` {R} 2nd attack, conditional `combat-detainment`, compound `attacks-not-detainment`) are shipped, and the filter context already exposes both `attack.automatic` and `enemy.race`. Data fix pass (recurring AS import bug): filled `automaticAttacks[0].creatureType` "orc", added `adjacentSites` (Mount Gundabad 0 / The Iron-deeps 5 / The Under-gates 5 / The Under-grottos 7 / The Under-vaults 6), and the three effects (was `[]`).
- Minion R&L site, pure reuse of the wound-corruption primitive (Haudh-in-Gwanûr le-380) — a plain twin of Barrow-downs (le-353): the single Undead automatic-attack (1 strike, prowess 10) carries the shipped `on-event: character-wounded-by-self` → `force-check` corruption (modifier -2) for "each character wounded must make a corruption check modified by -2". No engine change. Data fix: the imported entry had an empty `playableResources` (both the remaster `text` and its ES/FR translations read "Items (minor, major)", so the stale CoE `attributes.playable` "Items (minor)" was overridden) — set to `["minor","major"]`; sitePath {s}{w}{w} from Minas Morgul (le-390), 2/2 draws.

- `site-rule: deny-item` group exception + wound-corruption reuse (Lossadan Cairn le-388) — the sibling of Tolfalas (as-162 / hero tw-433), but its greater-item exception is a whole *keyword group* rather than a single named card. Two shipped primitives, no engine change: `on-event: character-wounded-by-self` → `force-check` corruption (modifier -2) for "each character wounded must make a corruption check modified by -2" (Barrow-downs le-353); and `site-rule: deny-item` for "greater* — Palantíri only" encoded as `{ "$and": [ { "subtype": "greater" }, { "$not": { "keywords": { "$includes": "palantir" } } } ] }` — greater items lacking the `palantir` keyword are denied, so The Arkenstone (greater, no keyword) is barred while every Palantír passes. `matchesDefinition` (`reducer-utils.ts`) evaluates the `when` against the full item def, and the condition-matcher already supports `$and`/`$not`/`$includes` on the array `keywords` field. **Data fix:** the authoritative cards.json tags all seven Palantíri (including the greater-subtype Amon Sûl / Annúminas / Osgiliath) with the `Palantír` keyword, but the imported game data had dropped it from the greater-subtype copies (le-330/331/335, tw-296/297/301, `keywords: []`) — restored to `["palantir"]` so the group exception resolves (same import bug class as Tharbad le-407 / Haudh-in-Gwanûr le-380; the `palantir` keyword is a valid enum in `types/common.ts` used by no engine gating, so the restore is behaviour-neutral). Everything else is structural: Undead auto-attack (2 strikes, 8 prowess), minor/major/greater playability, sitePath {s}{w} from Carn Dûm, 1/1 draws. No reveal-tap (contrast as-162).

- `cancel-deck-search` + persistent `creature-alt-event` permanent-event (Lady of the Golden Wood as-13) — the AS "hunter manifestation" dual creature/permanent-event pattern. Creature mode: 1 strike 12/10, keyed by region name to Wold & Foothills ("or at sites in this region" is covered by the region name appearing in the destination's site path, as-21 precedent), `combat-detainment` gated on hero / covert-FW defenders (as-8/as-21 shape). Permanent-event mode: `creature-alt-event` with the new `persistent: true` field — no Nazgûl-style tap-to-short-event conversion (`tapAltPermanentEventActions` skips it, `handleTapAltPermanentEvent` rejects); the card stays in play until "any play deck is exhausted" via the existing `on-event: play-deck-exhausted` self-discard (as-101/as-54 machinery). Its passive is the new `cancel-deck-search` effect: while in play, every `fetch-to-deck` pending enqueued for a minion/Balrog player has its `deck`/`discard-pile` sources stripped (sideboard arms survive; the fetch fizzles when nothing remains) — centralised in `gateDeckSearchFetch` (`reducer-utils.ts`), wired into the chain fetch path (le-253), the inline short-event path (le-162), `fetch-agent-to-hand` (dm-68), the end-of-turn both-players fetch (le-142), org-phase fetch (wh-61), and grant-action fetches (`grant-action-apply.ts`). "Manifestation of Galadriel" is `manifestId: "tw-153"`: the creature play path now checks unique-in-cardsInPlay + `manifestationOfEntityInPlay`, and the character play path uses the new `blockingManifestationForCharacterPlay` (`manifestations.ts`) so the in-play Lady bars playing Galadriel (the g.man.1 "would leave play" clause keeps The Balrog ba-3 / tw-12 working). `play-flag: playable-as-event` supplies the ½-creature deck weight (tw-2 precedent).

- Persistent `creature-alt-event` permanent-event + `on-event: end-of-turn` `actor: "both"` discard→hand recall (Master of the House as-16) — no new engine support was needed: the card composes three existing precedents. Creature mode is 1 strike 14/9 keyed by region name to Rhudaur / Arthedain / High Pass / The Shire / Cardolan ("or at sites in these regions" is covered by the destination site's own region appearing in `resolvedSitePathNames`, as-13/as-21 precedent), with `combat-detainment` gated on hero / covert-FW defenders (as-8/as-21 shape). Permanent-event mode is the as-13/as-14 `creature-alt-event` `persistent: true` shape (no tap-to-short-event conversion) plus the existing `on-event: play-deck-exhausted` self-discard. Its passive — "each player at the end of each turn may bring one Elf creature from his discard pile to his hand" — is the le-142 `on-event: end-of-turn` / `actor: "both"` shape scanned by `fireEndOfTurnFetchEffects` (`reducer-site.ts`, which scans **both** players' `cardsInPlay` and enqueues one optional `fetch-to-deck` pending effect per player) with td-27's `to: "hand"` destination on the `move` apply, filtered to `{ cardType: hazard-creature, race: elf }`; `moveToFetchToDeckPayload` already maps `to: 'hand'` through, and `fetchFromPileLegalActions` offers one `fetch-from-pile` per matching discard card plus `pass` for the "may". "Manifestation of Elrond" is `manifestId: "tw-145"` (as-13/as-14 g.man.1 wiring, both directions). `play-flag: playable-as-event` supplies the ½-creature deck weight (tw-2 precedent).

- `no-creatures-keyed-to-site` constraint kind with `unlessSiteRegionType` gate (Crack in the Wall le-177) — the inverse of Secret Passage's `only-creatures-keyed-to-site`: while active, hazard-creature plays against the protected company whose `keyedBy.method` is site-based (`site-type`, `site-name`, `site-keyword`, `adjacent-to-site-keyword`) are dropped; region-keyed plays of the same creature survive as their own actions (`applyNoCreaturesKeyedToSite`, `legal-actions/pending.ts`). The optional `unlessSiteRegionType` field (le-177: `"free"`) voids the restriction when the target company's destination site sits in a region of that type — resolved from the company's declared `destinationSite` via `siteRegionTypeOf` (`reducer-utils.ts`), so the gate tracks re-planned destinations. Added via the standard org-phase short-event `on-event: self-enters-play` → `add-constraint` path (`reducer-events.ts`), with parity in `buildConstraintKind` (`constraint-kind.ts`) for chain-played events. Play side reuses shipped primitives: `play-window` organization, `play-target` character filtered on sage/untapped/`company.moving` with a `tap: character` cost (Stealth/le-192 shape).

- Hero Under-deeps special item, pure reuse (Dragon-helm dm-167) — the hero twin of Ancient Black Axe (as-122), no engine change. `item-play-site` filter `{ under-deeps keyword AND siteType $in [dark-hold, shadow-hold] }` ("Playable at any Under-deeps Dark-hold or Shadow-hold"), three warrior-gated `stat-modifier`s (`when bearer.skills $includes warrior`): prowess +1, body +2 `max: 10`, direct-influence +3 — the "Warrior only:" clause covers only the stat bonuses. The tap-to-cancel is a separate, un-gated sentence: `cancel-attack` `cost: { tap: "self" }` (item taps, bearer status irrelevant — Helm of Fear as-126 shape) gated `enemy.race $in [dragon, dragons, drake, drakes]`, working against both hazard-creature and site automatic attacks (`combat.creatureRace` is normalized from auto-attack `creatureType`). `helmet` keyword added for rule-9.15 slot enforcement; structural `prowessModifier`/`bodyModifier` retained (ignored once a `stat-modifier` exists, tw-499 precedent).

- `no-character-play-limit` marker + Sauron manifestation (Sauron ba-43) — the sibling manifestation of The Lidless Eye (le-203), reusing its whole toolkit as data: `play-condition` player-state (opponent Wizard, no Ringwraith revealed), `play-as-sauron`, bare `general-influence +10` `stat-modifier`, `on-event self-enters-play` → `discard-named-in-play` "Bade to Rule" (le-167's existing `card-not-in-play: "Sauron"` condition supplies the "prevents subsequent play" half), the fetch-only `grant-action sauron-sideboard-fetch` (`sauronOrgGrantActions` handles fetch-only cards — no peek mode on ba-43), and `duplication-limit` scope `game`. Two new pieces: **(1)** `no-character-play-limit` — a marker effect on a bare permanent-event in `cardsInPlay` ("there is no limit to the number of characters you may bring into play"); `playerHasNoCharacterPlayLimit` (`reducer-utils.ts`, by effect type) is consumed by `playCharacterActions` (`organization-characters.ts`), which passes `characterPlayLimitReached: false` to the `one-character-per-turn` gate while the marker is in play — all other gates (influence, sites, uniqueness) still apply. **(2)** manifestation mutual exclusion (glossary g.man.1) as data: ba-43 carries `card-not-in-play: "The Lidless Eye"` and le-203 now carries the mirror `card-not-in-play: "Sauron"`, both honored by the generalized multi-condition loop in `playPermanentEventActions`. Deck inclusion: ba-43 sits in both `BALROG_BANNED_CARD_IDS` and `FALLEN_WIZARD_BANNED_CARD_IDS` (`deck-validation.ts`).

- `check-modifier` constraint `prowessSubstitution` (Threats le-244) — a one-shot faction-influence constraint payload (`{ "max": N }` on the `add-constraint check-modifier` apply, no `value` needed) that **replaces** the influencer's unused direct influence with his prowess: when the faction-influence check consumes the constraint, the whole unused-DI contribution (free `availableDI` plus conditional `direct-influence` stat-modifiers) is backed out and `min(effective prowess, max)` added instead. Prowess is read at *resolution* time from `effectiveStats.prowess` (CRF 22: "your prowess is calculated when it resolves"), not baked at play time like Muster's `valueExpr`. Honoured at all three influence seams: influence-attempt need display (`legal-actions/site.ts`), pending faction-influence-roll need (`legal-actions/pending.ts`), and the roll resolver + constraint consumption (`reducer-site.ts`). The play-target pins the card to the influencing warrior via `$and` of `target.skills $includes warrior` and `target.isInfluencing` (The Dark Power as-79 shape); the play-option rides the chain like every influence booster (Muster tw-288 shape, `when player.hasFactionInHand`). Used by Threats (le-244): "Warrior only. Playable on a warrior attempting to influence a faction. Warrior does not use his unused direct influence for the attempt. Instead he uses his prowess, to a maximum modifier of +6."

- `force-check-all-in-play` + corruption-check sequencing fields (Ren the Unclean tw-83) — the fourth Nazgûl dual creature/permanent-event (tw-2/tw-47/tw-107 shape: `{d}{D}` base keying + `regionNames` alt keying, `play-flag: playable-as-event`, `creature-alt-event` mode permanent-event, tap during opponent's M/H via `tap-alt-permanent-event` counting one against the hazard limit, plus the dm-36 "any Nazgûl permanent-event" `permanent-event-auto-attack` — Nazgûl 1×15/10, `discardAfterUse`; no dm-35 entry, that site names only Khamûl/Adûnaphel). Its on-tap short-event carries the new **`force-check-all-in-play`** effect: `applyForceCheckAllInPlay` (`chain-reducer.ts`) enqueues one `corruption-check` pending resolution per character in play (both players, actor = controller, scope M/H phase), realising the printed sequencing through three new fields on the `corruption-check` pending kind plus one on `PendingResolution`: **`blockedBy`** (generic scheduling gate — `topResolutionFor` skips an entry while any listed resolution ID is still queued; the non-moving player's checks are blocked by every moving-player check ID: "the moving player makes corruption checks first"), **`selectableOrder`** (the actor resolves their same-source checks in any order — `corruptionCheckActions` emits one roll action per selectable sibling and the apply half swaps head for the matching sibling: "each player decides the order"), **`allowSupport`** (mid-game CoE 7.1.1 tap-in-support: `support-corruption-check` — now carrying an optional `targetCharacterId` — taps an untapped company mate and adds a one-shot +1 corruption `check-modifier` constraint the roll re-reads and consumes), and **`noResourceAid`** (suppresses reactive resource short-event hand plays for the tapping player's checks — "you cannot play resources to aid your character's corruption checks" — while in-play grant activations stay legal). Effect fields `declarerMayTapSupport` / `declarerNoResourceAid` scope the last two to the declaring (tapping) player's own checks.

- `cancel-hazard-event-play` + `hazard-limit-environment` extensions (`floor`, `appliesTo: "all"`, optional `when`) + long-event `play-condition` player-state gate (The Great Eye as-85) — three pieces behind the Sauron long-event. **(1)** `cancel-hazard-event-play` is a marker on an in-play card: during any `normal`-restriction chain, `cancelHazardEventChainActions` (`legal-actions/chain.ts`) offers the priority player one `cancel-hazard-event` action per (in-play source, target) pair, where a target is an unresolved opponent-declared entry whose card is a `hazard-event` (short/long/permanent — creatures excluded) and whose payload does not carry `fromOnGuard`. `handleCancelHazardEvent` (`chain-reducer.ts`) discards the source from `cardsInPlay` (the cost), negates the entry, and flips priority; the canceled card reaches its owner's discard via `completeChain`'s negated-entry flush, which now **skips instances already in a discard pile** (short events are discarded at play time — prevents duplicating the instance; also fixes the latent double-flush after a grant-action `cancel-chain-entry`). The new `fromOnGuard: true` payload marker is set by both on-guard reveal paths (`handleChainRevealOnGuard` in `chain-reducer.ts`, `applyOnGuardRevealAtResource` in `reducer-site.ts`), realising "this cannot be used against an on-guard card". **(2)** `hazard-limit-environment` (dm-56) gains `floor` (a negative `value` never reduces the limit below it, and never raises a limit already at/below it), `appliesTo: "all"` (stationary companies too; default `"moving"` keeps dm-56 semantics), and an optional `when` (absent = every company) — `snapshotHazardLimit` (`mh-steps.ts`). **(3)** the resource **long-event** play path (`longEventActions`, `legal-actions/long-event.ts`) now honours `play-condition` `requires: "player-state"` (the as-79/as-82 gate, `buildPlayerStateContext`), so "Playable if you are Sauron" holds for long events. Used by The Great Eye (as-85): play-condition player-state (`player.playsAsSauron`) + `hazard-limit-environment` `value: -1, floor: 2, appliesTo: "all"` + `cancel-hazard-event-play` + `duplication-limit` scope game.

- `cancel-chain-entry` `select: "target"` with generic `filter` + `removeFromGame` (Ire of the East wh-24) — extends the Searching Eye chain-response shape beyond `requiredSkill`: the apply may carry a `filter` condition evaluated per unresolved chain entry against `{ target: { cardType, eventType, name }, declaredBy: { alignment } }` (`playSkillCancelChainActions`, `legal-actions/chain.ts` — a hazard short-event in hand is offered as a `play-short-event` chain response whenever a matching entry is live, in any phase; the reducer path is the shared `handlePlayShortEvent` → chain push → `resolveEnvironmentCancel` negation). wh-24's filter is `{ target.cardType: "minion-resource-event", target.eventType: "short", declaredBy.alignment: "fallen-wizard" }` ("one minion short-event played by a Fallen-wizard earlier in the same chain"). `removeFromGame: true` on the apply moves the spent event from its player's discard pile to their out-of-play pile when its own entry resolves un-negated (`resolveEntry`, `chain-reducer.ts` — ba-16 disposal precedent); a negated entry leaves it in discard. "Does not count against the hazard limit" is the chain-response path (never counted) plus `play-flag: no-hazard-limit` documenting the clause. Blind to the West (wh-14) is the hero-side twin, certified on the same primitive with no engine change: identical effects with `target.cardType: "hero-resource-event"` ("one hero short-event played by a Fallen-wizard"), so the pair demonstrates the filter discriminating on card type alone — wh-24 is not offered against Concealment and wh-14 is not offered against Orc Quarrels.

- `on-event: successful-influence-attempt` (Lure of Power tw-59) — fires when a **character** completes a successful influence attempt, from BOTH influence-success seams: the faction influence roll (`resolveInfluenceAttemptRoll`) and the opponent-influence resolution (`resolveOpponentInfluenceDefend`), matching the card's own examples ("against a faction, an opponent's character, etc."). Carried by a bare in-play event in either player's `cardsInPlay` and scanned by `fireSuccessfulInfluenceTriggers` (`reducer-site.ts`); the `when` is matched against `{ target: { race, name } }` of the influencing character (an ally influencing "as if a character", wh-114, never triggers — the text says "character"). The apply is a `sequence` of `enqueue-corruption-check` (Site-phase pending check on the influencer, so it resolves "immediately" before any further action) and a self-discard `move` ("discard this card after this corruption check"). Duplicate copies of the same definition fire only ONE corruption check but are ALL discarded, per the card's official clarification. Used by Lure of Power (tw-59): "The next non-Hobbit character to make a successful influence attempt … must immediately make a corruption check modified by -4. Discard this card after this corruption check." — `when target.race $ne hobbit`, modifier -4.

- Avatar-targeting corruption permanent-event, Lure-shape reuse + per-controller `bearer.atHaven` (Longing for the West wh-25) — the Lure of the Senses (tw-60) package inverted: `play-target` character filtered `target.race $in [wizard, fallen-wizard]`, `duplication-limit` scope character, `stat-modifier` corruption-points +2, `on-event: untap-phase-end` → `force-check` corruption gated `when: { "bearer.atHaven": false }` ("…if not at a Haven [{H}] (or Wizardhaven)"), and the tap-to-roll removal `grant-action` `remove-self-on-roll` (threshold 7). One engine generalization: the untap-phase-end bearer context (`reducer-untap.ts` `advanceToOrganization`) now computes `bearer.atHaven` via `isHavenForPlayer` with the active player's alignment and the `wizardhaven-conversion` lookup, instead of a bare `siteType === 'haven'` check — so a Fallen-wizard bearer counts his Wizardhavens (FW-alignment haven sites and Hidden-Haven-style converted sites) as "{H}", matching the per-controller {H} reading used elsewhere in the engine. Hero/minion bearers are unchanged (any haven-class site).

- `on-event: stage-card-played` + bearer-conditional `stage-points` + `bearer.stagePoints` in effective-stats (Inner Rot wh-23) — the Longing for the West (wh-25) package (character `play-target` filtered `target.race $in [wizard, fallen-wizard]`, `duplication-limit` scope character, corruption-point `stat-modifier`, tap-to-roll `grant-action remove-self-on-roll` threshold 7) plus three generalizations for the Fallen-wizard stage track. **(1)** `stage-points` may now carry a `when` and is summed from a character's **`hazards`** slot as well as `items`, so an opponent's hazard can push stage points onto the Fallen-wizard it rides (`playerStagePoints`, `recompute-derived.ts`; `stagePointsOfCard` takes an optional context and skips unmatched conditional effects — a bare in-play card has no bearer, so `{ "bearer.race": "fallen-wizard" }` never fires from `cardsInPlay`). **(2)** The player's stage-point total is computed **before** the per-character effective-stats pass (extracted into `playerStagePoints`, which reads only definitions/zones/site occupancy — no cycle) and exposed as `bearer.stagePoints`, so a card can tier its own modifiers on the total it just contributed to. wh-23's "use the first that applies" ladder is four corruption-point `stat-modifier`s over disjoint half-open bands (`$gt 18`, `11 < x <= 18`, `5 < x <= 11`, `0 < x <= 5`, each `$and`-ed with `bearer.race = fallen-wizard`); note a single operator object evaluates only its *first* operator, so a two-sided band must be two `$and` terms. A fifth flat `+1` is gated `bearer.race = wizard` for the hero-Wizard reading. **(3)** New `on-event: stage-card-played` (`stage-card-played.ts`) fires whenever a player brings a card with `alignment: "stage"` into play, scanning that player's characters' attached items/hazards and enqueuing a phase-scoped corruption check on the bearer via `force-check`. Wired at all three entry seams — permanent-event chain resolution (`chain-reducer.ts`) and the site-phase item/ally attach + successful faction-influence paths (`reducer-site.ts`) — so the 5 non-event stage cards (wh-86/87/88/89/114) trigger it too; a faction whose influence fails never enters play and never triggers.

- Character-attached **negative** `general-influence` / `hand-size-modifier` with a `when`, and character-attached `play-deck-exhausted` self-discard (Cruel Claw Perceived wh-16) — three generalizations, all reusing shipped effect types rather than adding new ones. **(1)** The general-influence collection pass (`recompute-derived.ts`, `applyGeneralInfluenceEffect`) now scans a character's **`hazards`** slot alongside `items`, and honors the effect's `when` against a bearer context (`buildBearerContext` + the player's `stagePoints`, already computed earlier in `recomputePlayer`). So a hazard on the opponent's avatar can shrink *his* pool, and one card can carry a whole ladder of alternatives. wh-16's ladder is a flat `-1` gated `bearer.race $in [wizard, ringwraith]` plus four Fallen-wizard bands over disjoint half-open `bearer.stagePoints` ranges (`$gt 20`, `15 < x <= 20`, `10 < x <= 15`, `5 < x <= 10`) — "this modifier is **instead**", so the ladder replaces the -1 rather than stacking, and a Fallen-wizard at 5 SPs or fewer falls off the bottom and takes no modifier at all (same shape as Inner Rot wh-23's corruption ladder). Bare `cardsInPlay` cards get an empty bearer context, so a bearer-gated `when` never fires there. **(2)** `resolveHandSize` (`effects/resolver.ts`) now puts `stagePoints` in its bearer context, so a `hand-size-modifier` can tier on the Fallen-wizard's stage track; negative values simply sum, which is how wh-16's two independent `-1`s ("reduced by 1 if his SPs exceed 10, and by 1 more if his SPs exceed 20") stack to `-2` above 20. **(3)** "Discard when any play deck is exhausted" is pure reuse of the character-attached `play-deck-exhausted` sweep shipped with Fool's Bane (wh-19): `completeDeckExhaust` (`reducer-utils.ts`) already discards attachments carrying `on-event: play-deck-exhausted` + a self-discard `move` from a character's `items`/`hazards` slot to their own owner's pile, on *any* player's exhaustion.
- `retain-hazard-long-events` + `inPlayAnywhere` in the player-state context (The Will of Sauron tw-100) — a game-wide hazard permanent-event that freezes the hazard long-event lifecycle. **(1)** New `retain-hazard-long-events` effect (no fields), implemented in `engine/retain-hazard-long-events.ts` in two halves: `hazardLongEventsRetained(state)` is a single-state query (a carrier in **either** player's `cardsInPlay`, skipping `pendingTriggerAttack` and set-aside cards) consulted by the long-event phase handler (`reducer-events.ts` `handleLongEvent`) before its [2.III.3] sweep — while a carrier is in play the sweep is skipped and hazard long-events accumulate across turns; `sweepRetainedHazardLongEvents(prev, next)` is a `postReduce` prev/next diff (the `discard-on-card-leaves-play` reactive-diff pattern) that discards every `hazard-event` `eventType: "long"` from **both** players' `cardsInPlay` when the last carrier just left play. It is wired into `postReduce` (`reducer.ts`) *after* the single-state sweeps, so a carrier discarded by `sweepDiscardSelfWhen` in the same step is already absent from the compared state — the Gates of Morning → Doors of Night → Will of Sauron → hazard long-events cascade resolves in a single action. Being a diff, it fires however the carrier left (self-discard, deck exhaustion, cancellation). **(2)** `buildPlayerStateContext` (`legal-actions/organization.ts`) gains `inPlayAnywhere` — the game-wide in-play name list (`buildInPlayNames`, honouring `environment-override`) beside the controller-scoped `inPlay` — so `discard-self-when` / `play-condition player-state` can express "if &lt;named card&gt; is not in play" regardless of who played it. tw-100's remaining rules reuse shipped machinery: `play-condition requires: card-in-play` `cardName: "Doors of Night"` (the Snowstorm tw-91 gate, enforced in the M/H long/permanent-event branch), `discard-self-when` `{ "$not": { "inPlayAnywhere": "Doors of Night" } }`, `on-event: play-deck-exhausted` self-discard `move` (fired by `completeDeckExhaust`), and `duplication-limit` scope `game` max 1.

- `play-condition requires: card-in-play` on **resource permanent-events** + player-wide effect collection from character-attached cards (Oromë's Warders wh-94) — two generalizations, no new effect type. **(1)** `playPermanentEventActions` (`legal-actions/organization-events.ts`) now honours the `card-in-play` gate, checked ahead of every play-target branch so it applies to bare, site-, character-, company-, and faction-targeting permanent-events alike. **Every** such condition on the card must hold (`findPlayConditionEffects`, the plural sibling), and each is evaluated against the playing player's **own** play area with `isCardNameInPlayForPlayer` — attachment-aware (`cardsInPlay`, his characters, and the items/hazards they bear) so a prerequisite that itself sits on the avatar counts, and an opponent's copy never satisfies "if … is in play". An unmet gate emits `not-playable` naming the missing card. **(2)** `playerInPlayAndCharacterDefs` (`recompute-derived.ts`) — the source list behind `faction-mp-override` and the three `fw-*-mp-full` exemptions — now also scans the `items` attached to the player's characters, because a stage permanent-event played "on the avatar" lives there rather than in `cardsInPlay` (the same place `mpOverrideRules` already scanned for wh-99). Used by Oromë's Warders (wh-94), the follow-up stage to Join the Hunt (wh-93): `play-condition card-in-play` "Join the Hunt" + `play-target` `character` `{ target.name: "Alatar" }` ("Playable on Alatar if Join the Hunt is in play"), `fw-mp-full (cards: items)` and `fw-mp-full (cards: allies)` carrying wh-93's filters but **player-wide** (no `inAvatarCompany` — "your … items/allies", not "in Alatar's company"), `faction-mp-override` with the single rule `{ "faction.race": "elf" } → 2`, and `stage-points` 3.

- `cancel-deck-search` `affects: "all"` + per-acting-player `when` gate (Flotsam and Jetsam wh-18) — the third member of the as-13 / tw-13 search-cancel family, and the first that picks its victims by **deck size** rather than by alignment. Two small extensions to `deckSearchCancellerFor` (`reducer-utils.ts`): `affects` gains `"all"` (every player, whatever his alignment), and the effect's inherited `when` is evaluated once per *acting* player against `{ player: { alignment, minion, playDeckSize } }` before the card is treated as a canceller. Everything downstream is unchanged — the single `gateDeckSearchFetch` chokepoint still strips `deck`/`discard-pile` sources at all six fetch seams, sideboard arms survive, and a fetch left with no source fizzles. "If a player has 15 or fewer cards in his play deck (20 or fewer if a Fallen-wizard)" is therefore pure data: an `$or` over `{ player.alignment $ne fallen-wizard, player.playDeckSize $lte 15 }` and `{ player.alignment = fallen-wizard, player.playDeckSize $lte 20 }`. Because the gate is re-read at each search, a player slides under the cancel mid-game as his deck runs down, and the two players can be on opposite sides of it at the same time. The remaining two rules are shipped machinery: `on-event: play-deck-exhausted` self-discard `move` (fired by `completeDeckExhaust`) and `duplication-limit` scope `game`.

- `nullify-influence-modifications` (Webs of Fear & Treachery le-150) — a game-wide environment effect on a bare in-play event that strips every **card-sourced** modification from every influence attempt by either player. `influenceModificationsNullified` (`reducer-utils.ts`) scans both players' bare `cardsInPlay` (the `collectGlobalCheckModifier` scan shape); every influence-check computation consults it: the faction-influence need and the opponent-influence display (`legal-actions/site.ts`), the paused faction-influence roll (`legal-actions/pending.ts`), the faction roll resolver and the opponent-influence attempt (`reducer-site.ts`), and the rule 10.14 agent attempt (`mh-agents.ts`). **Survives:** the 2d6 roll(s), the printed target value, unused **general** influence (including a `generalInfluenceSubstitution` override, whose value *is* unused GI), unused **normal** direct influence, and rules-level modifications (cross-alignment penalty, rule 10.14 home-site bonuses); the defender's roll is untouched (Alfano, Worlds 2009). **Normal unused DI** is the new `normalUnusedDI` (`legal-actions/organization.ts`): printed `directInfluence` plus the `direct-influence` modifiers on the influencer's **own** card (the `sourceInstance === <influencer>` slice of `collectCharacterEffects` — "influence modifications given in a character's card text"), minus followers' mind cost via the extracted `followersMindCost`; deliberately NOT `effectiveStats.directInfluence`, so DI granted by a ring or a permanent-event played on him is nullified like any other card's text. **Zeroed:** all other influence `check-modifier` / `direct-influence` `stat-modifier` effects (items, attached hazards, allies, `player-in-play`, `all-in-play`), a faction's printed Standard Modifications, one-shot influence constraints (Muster, `prowessSubstitution`, opponent-influence boosters) — still **consumed** by the attempt but worth 0 — player/site/game-wide influence constraints, `faction-influence-restriction` environments, the Prophet of Doom region penalty, and paid `influence-modification` bonuses (no longer offered, so no item is discarded for nothing).

- `site-phase-start-attack` + `company-movement-roll` + `lock-company-movement` dice-check verb (Siege tw-87) — the two ongoing rules of a card that **besieges a site**, both read off a card in play bound to a site location (`CardInPlay.attachedToSite`) by the new `siteStartOfPhaseAttacks` / `siteMovementRolls` collectors (`reducer-utils.ts`, scanning both players' `cardsInPlay`). **(1) `site-phase-start-attack`** — the company faces the effect's attack *at the beginning of its site phase*, before the enter-or-skip decision, so doing nothing at the site does not avoid it (the French printing states this explicitly: "La compagnie doit affronter l'attaque du Siège avant de choisir si elle explore le site"). A new `siege-attacks` {@link SiteStep} sits between `select-company` and `enter-or-skip`: `handleSiteSelectCompany` (`reducer-site.ts`) enters it and initiates the first attack, `handleSiteSiegeAttacks` sequences the rest one per `pass` (the `troll-purse-attacks` / `rescue-attacks` pattern, progress in `SitePhaseState.siegeAttacks`), and `legal-actions/site.ts` routes the step through `automaticAttacksActions`. The combat carries a new `siege-attack` `AttackSource` and is deliberately **not** an automatic-attack — `detainment` is false, no auto-attack prowess/duplicate constraint applies, the home-site tap-to-cancel is not offered, and finalization disposes of nothing (the besieging card stays in play). **(2) `company-movement-roll`** — `handleOrganizationPass` (`reducer-organization.ts`) enqueues one generic `dice-check` per besieged company of the active player *after* the phase transition, scoped to the following long-event phase; pending resolutions outrank phase actions, so the rolls resolve before anything else. The penalty (`penaltyPerCharacterWithoutSkill`, `penalty` default 1) is a `constant` modifier computed at enqueue from `getEffectiveSkills`. `onFail` runs the new `lock-company-movement` `dice-check` branch verb (`pending-reducers.ts`), which reads the new `dice-check` field `targetCompanyId`, calls the extracted `clearPlannedMovement` helper (shared with the `cancel-movement` action) to drop the declared destination back into the location deck, and installs a turn-scoped `company-cannot-move` constraint (the Hide in Dark Places le-192 kind, so re-declaring is barred too). **(3)** `discardOrphanedSiteAttachedEvents` (`reducer-utils.ts`) now counts a company's declared `destinationSite` as occupying that site location alongside `currentSite` — without it any site-targeting hazard permanent-event played during the M/H phase (Siege, Troll-purse dm-95) was swept as an orphan on the very action that played it, since the destination only becomes `currentSite` at M/H step 8. **(4)** the M/H site-targeting play path (`legal-actions/movement-hazard.ts`) now honours `duplication-limit` scope `site` via `countPermanentEventCopiesAtSite` ("Cannot be duplicated on a given site"). tw-87's remaining clauses reuse shipped machinery: `play-target` `target: "site"` with `filter` `siteType $in [border-hold, free-hold]`, and "Discard when the site card is discarded or returned to the location deck" = the site-attached orphan sweep (the card claims no `cardKeepsBoundSitePermanent` exemption).

- `cancel-strike` `cost: { check: "corruption", modifier: N }` + ring-test uniqueness gate (The One Ring tw-347) — the corruption-check cost variant of `cancel-strike`: instead of tapping, the bearer makes a corruption check (modifier `N`) to cancel the current strike against himself. Offered by `selfCancelStrikeActions` (`legal-actions/combat.ts`) regardless of the source item's tapped status (a check is not a tap), gated by the effect's `when` against the usual cancel-strike context (`bearer`, `attack`, `enemy.race` — tw-347 excludes `undead`/`ringwraith` strikes); paid in `handleCancelStrike` (`combat-actions.ts`), which enqueues the corruption check and cancels the strike unconditionally (the check is the risk, not a condition). Playability of a `special`-subtype ring needs no extra gate — no site lists `special` in `playableResources`, so the only route into play is a gold-ring test whose `ring-test-table` category matches the ring's keyword (`the-one-ring`).
