"war-forges-item-unlocked", "scope": "turn" } }`. As a bearer-less,
site-attached source (no `companyId`) it rides the existing
`bareCardGrantActions` emitter unchanged (Earth-eater wh-67 precedent); the
new part is `handleInPlayCardGrantAction`'s (`grant-action-apply.ts`)
`add-constraint` resolution, which previously only built payload-free
constraint kinds for bearer-less sources — a new branch resolves the
`war-forges-item-unlocked` site-flag's `siteDefinitionId` from the **source
card's own** `CardInPlay.attachedToSite` (its site binding from its own
`play-target: "site"` play) rather than from a bearer's company (there is no
bearer). While the resulting `scope: "turn"` constraint is active,
`playResourcesActions` (`legal-actions/site.ts`) offers one non-hoard
(`!keywords.includes('hoard')`), non-unique, `minor`-subtype item at the
bound site whether tapped or untapped — bypassing the tapped-site gate and
the item's own `item-play-site` restriction exactly like
`technology-item-unlocked`, gated on subtype/uniqueness/keyword instead of
the Technology keyword. `SitePhaseState.warForgesItemPlayed` tracks the
one-per-site-phase consumption (mirrors `technologyItemPlayed`); the played
item leaves the site untapped and does not count as the company's tapping
resource (`reducer-site.ts`, `siteHasWarForgesItemUnlock` helper in
`reducer-utils.ts`). Cleared by `discardOrphanedSiteAttachedEvents` when the
bound site leaves play (the card carries no `starting-item` keyword text
implications beyond the standard site-attached lifecycle).

"The item may be taken from your discard pile or sideboard" sources the
bonus item from three places, offered by a dedicated loop (mirroring Glove of
Radagast wh-111's discard-sourced ally loop) that iterates
`player.discardPile` and `player.sideboard` for matching items, in addition
to the ordinary hand-item loop gaining the same tapped-site/site-restriction
bypass. The discard-pile source reuses the existing `fromDiscard`
`play-hero-resource` flag (already generic over items and allies in
`handleSitePlayHeroResource`); the sideboard source needed a new
`PlayHeroResourceAction.fromSideboard` flag (`actions-site.ts`) — no prior
`play-hero-resource` path read from the sideboard. `handleSitePlayHeroResource`
resolves the source card from `player.sideboard` and removes it from there
(hand and discard pile untouched) when the flag is set.

Used by *War-forges* (wh-83): "Playable on one of your protected Wizardhavens
[{H}] (not by Radagast). You may tap War-forges to make an additional
non-hoard, non-unique minor item playable at this site this turn (if the site
is tapped or not). The item may be taken from your discard pile or sideboard.
Discard when this site is discarded or returned to your location deck.
Cannot be duplicated on a given site." — `duplication-limit` scope `site`.

- `tap-character` gains `requiresCompanionSkill` + multi-mode support (Gnaw with Words dm-60) — extends the New Moon (tw-68, §above) `tap-character` primitive with a dynamic per-candidate gate and lets a card carry **more than one** `tap-character` effect for "Alternatively" modes that target different skills. `requiresCompanionSkill: Skill` requires some *other* character carrying that skill in the candidate's own company, at its company's current site, or at its destination site (if moving) — evaluated by the new `hasNearbySkillmate` helper (`legal-actions/movement-hazard.ts`): company membership only scans the candidate's own company-mates (same player), while site presence is checked by site *name* across **both** players' companies (the same "at the same site" convention `enqueue-site-wound-rolls`/Plague le-129 uses). The eligibility loop now collects all `tap-character` effects on the card (`.filter` instead of `.find`) and offers a candidate once it satisfies *any one* mode's `filter` + `requiresCompanionSkill` pair — the modes are pure alternatives, so a character qualifying under both still gets one action (the resolved tap is identical either way). Used by *Gnaw with Words* (dm-60): "Tap a sage if another sage is in his company or at his current site or at his new site. Alternatively, tap a diplomat if another diplomat is in his company or at his current site or at his new site." — two `tap-character` effects, `filter: { skills: { $includes: "sage" } }` / `requiresCompanionSkill: "sage"` and the diplomat equivalent.

### `dragonAtHomeVictorySiteId` + `siteFrom` + `dwarf-hold-override` + `item-play-race-restriction` — "if his company has defeated an at-home Dragon manifestation" (King under the Mountain td-126)

King under the Mountain (td-126) is "Playable on Balin, Dáin II, Thorin II, or Thráin II if his company has defeated an at home Dragon manifestation attack other than Eärcaraxë at Home. The target Dwarf receives +5 direct influence against Dwarves and Dwarf factions. The site where the Dragon was defeated becomes a Border-hold [{B}] and Dwarf-hold for all purposes and has no Dragon automatic-attacks. Only Dwarves may play items at this site." — four new pieces, all **fully implemented**:

- **`CharacterInPlay.dragonAtHomeVictorySiteId`** (`types/state-cards.ts`) — a new persistent (never-cleared) field, the counterpart of `woundedByRaceThisTurn`'s "recorded in combat-finalize, queried via a `play-target` filter" shape but permanent rather than turn-scoped. `collectAtHomeAttacks` (`manifestations.ts`) now stamps `sourceInstanceId: card.instanceId` on the augmented attack it contributes (mirroring `collectPermanentEventAttacks`), so `finalizeCombat` (`combat-finalize.ts`, alongside the pre-existing `onDefeat: 'remove-from-play'` block) can tell, when a site automatic-attack is fully defeated, whether the defeated attack's source card carries a `dragon-at-home` effect — i.e. it is the *augmented* attack an in-play "\<Dragon\> at Home" permanent-event contributes, not the lair's baseline printed Dragon attack. If so (and the source card is not Eärcaraxë at Home, td-22, excluded by definition id), every member of the defending company (`combat.companyId`) is stamped with the site's definition id. Exposed to `play-target` filters as `target.dragonAtHomeVictorySiteId` in **both** character-targeting contexts a permanent event can be played from — the organization-phase emitter (`organization-events.ts`) and the site-phase `play-resources` emitter (`legal-actions/site.ts`, a separate context-builder for the same `play-target: "character"` filter) — a filter added to only one would silently fail in the other.
- **`siteFrom: "dragon-at-home-victory"`** (`AddConstraintAction`, `types/effects.ts`) — a character-targeted permanent event's `on-event: self-enters-play → add-constraint` normally binds a site-scoped constraint to the active company's current site (or the card's own `targetSiteDefinitionId` play target). This flag overrides that resolution: `applyAddConstraintFromOnEvent` (`chain-reducer.ts`) looks up the play-target character's own `dragonAtHomeVictorySiteId` and threads it through as `boundSiteDefId`, so "the site where the Dragon was defeated" — a site with no relationship to where the card itself is played — becomes the bound site for every one of the card's site-scoped `add-constraint` effects.
- **`dwarf-hold-override`** (new `SiteFlag`, `types/pending.ts` + `constraint-kind.ts` `SITE_BOUND_FLAGS`) — a site-bound boolean marker, structurally identical to `skip-automatic-attacks`. Its sole consumer, `buildGrantActionContext`'s `site.keywords` (`legal-actions/organization.ts` — the same context Map to Mithril td-133 reads `dwarf-hold` from), now folds in the literal `'dwarf-hold'` tag via `hasSiteFlag` alongside the site's printed `keywords`, so "becomes … a Dwarf-hold for all purposes" reads as true to the one existing rule gated on that keyword. `skip-automatic-attacks` itself (pre-existing) supplies "has no Dragon automatic-attacks": every Dragon's-lair site in the pool has exactly one printed automatic-attack (the Dragon), so skipping all of them at the bound site is exactly "no Dragon automatic-attacks."
- **`item-play-race-restriction`** (new `ActiveConstraint` kind, `types/pending.ts`) — `{ siteDefinitionId, race }`, built by a new `buildConstraintKind` case (`constraint-kind.ts`, resolving `explicitSiteDefId` first like `site-type-override`) and consumed directly by the item-play candidate-character filter in `legal-actions/site.ts` (the `itemEligibleCharacters` computation, narrowed to characters whose race matches when a matching constraint binds the site) — a pass-through `case` in `applyOneConstraint`'s `legal-actions/pending.ts` switch, mirroring `item-play-corruption-check`.

Together with `site-type-override` (`overrideType: "border-hold"`, no `purpose` — a full override, the as-88 Hold Rebuilt and Repaired shape), td-126 declares five `on-event: self-enters-play` effects (the +5 direct-influence bonus itself is an ordinary bearer-scoped `stat-modifier` pair, the Arkenstone tw-341 shape, since the card enters play as an item on its target character). Card: `play-target` character (`target.name` in the four named Dwarfs, AND `target.dragonAtHomeVictorySiteId` exists) + two `stat-modifier` (`reason: "influence-check"` / `"faction-influence-check"`, `target.race`/`faction.race: "dwarf"`, value 5) + four `add-constraint` `on-event`s (`site-type-override`, `dwarf-hold-override`, `skip-automatic-attacks`, `item-play-race-restriction`), each carrying `siteFrom: "dragon-at-home-victory"`.

Used by *King under the Mountain* (td-126).

### `company-stat-modifier` gains an optional `max` cap (Miruvor tw-283)

Miruvor (tw-283): "Discard to give +2 body (to a maximum of 10) for all
characters in bearer's company until the end of the turn." — the existing
Orc-draughts-style `grant-action` → `add-constraint constraint:
"company-stat-modifier"` shape (`cost: { discard: "self" }`, `target:
"bearer-company"`, `scope: "turn"`) had no way to express a ceiling on the
resulting stat, unlike the sibling `character-stat-modifier` kind (Biter and
Beater! as-46) which already carried one. Three call sites now thread an
optional `max` end to end:

- `AddConstraintAction.max` (`types/effects.ts`) — the JSON `apply` clause may
  carry `"max": 10` alongside `stat`/`value`.
- `ActiveConstraint.kind` for `company-stat-modifier` (`types/pending.ts`)
  gained an optional `max` field; `buildPayloadConstraintKind`
  (`grant-action-apply.ts`) copies `apply.max` onto it when present.
- `collectCompanyStatModifierEffects` (`effects/resolver.ts`) forwards
  `constraint.kind.max` onto the synthesized `stat-modifier` effect, so the
  existing `resolveStatModifiers` cap logic (`if (effect.max !== undefined)
  result = Math.min(result, maxVal)`) applies exactly as it does for a
  JSON-declared item bonus. Each stacked copy of the source item is capped
  independently (its own `+2` clamped to 10), matching the printed "to a
  maximum of 10" reading rather than a single company-wide ceiling.

```json
{ "type": "grant-action", "action": "company-body-boost", "anyPhase": true,
  "cost": { "discard": "self" },
  "apply": { "type": "add-constraint", "constraint": "company-stat-modifier",
             "stat": "body", "value": 2, "max": 10,
             "target": "bearer-company", "scope": "turn" } }
```

Used by *Miruvor* (tw-283).

### `dragonAtHomeVictorySiteIds` + `site.dragonAtHomeVictory` + `bearer.itemNames` — "a site where an at home Dragon manifestation was defeated" (Returned Exiles td-146)

Returned Exiles (td-146) is "Playable at a tapped or untapped site where an at home Dragon manifestation was defeated if the influence check is greater than 12. Standard Modifications: King under the Mountain Dwarf (+5), other Dwarves (+2)." Its playability condition looks like King under the Mountain's (td-126, above) but is fundamentally different in shape: it must hold for *any* site, queryable at faction-playability time with no bearing character in scope, and must survive the winning company disbanding or dying — `dragonAtHomeVictorySiteId` (singular, per-character) cannot answer it. Three new pieces, all **fully implemented**:

- **`GameState.dragonAtHomeVictorySiteIds`** (`types/state.ts`) — a new persistent (never-cleared) array of site definition ids, the `singletonTapLocks` shape (`types/state.ts`, "recorded here, never cleared") applied to sites instead of card names. `finalizeCombat` (`combat-finalize.ts`) appends the site's definition id here in the same `isDragonAtHome` block that already stamps `CharacterInPlay.dragonAtHomeVictorySiteId` for td-126 — but *unconditionally* (no Eärcaraxë-at-Home exclusion, since td-146's text carries none) and regardless of whether the defending company still exists afterward. Initialized to `[]` in `init.ts` alongside `singletonTapLocks`.
- **`site.dragonAtHomeVictory`** (`siteMatchesEntry`, `reducer-utils.ts`) — a new boolean in the `when`-condition context `PlayableAtAny`/`PlayableAtSite`/`PlayableAtSiteType` entries are matched against, alongside the pre-existing `site.name`/`siteType`/`regionType`/`region`/`keywords`/`autoAttack`/`lairOf`/`isUnderDeepsSurface`. `siteMatchesEntry` gained a 6th parameter (`dragonAtHomeVictory = false`); every call site (the faction/ally site-phase play-legality checks and the Crown-of-Flowers pairing check in `legal-actions/organization.ts`) now threads it through a new `siteHasDragonAtHomeVictory(state, siteDefId)` helper (`reducer-utils.ts`) that looks the site id up in the array above. Returned Exiles declares `"playableAt": [{ "any": true, "when": { "site.dragonAtHomeVictory": true } }]` — the `PlayableAtAny` shape A Panoply of Wings (wh-37) established, with a condition this new field answers.
- **`bearer.itemNames`** (`ResolverContext.bearer`, `engine/effects/resolver.ts`) — a new array of item names (including character-attached permanent events, which live in `CharacterInPlay.items` alongside true items — see `keyword-replaced.ts`'s "there is no `attachedToCharacter` zone"), populated only in the faction-influence-check bearer context (`legal-actions/site.ts`). Lets a faction's own printed Standard Modification target a named-card holder rather than a race/keyword: Returned Exiles' "King under the Mountain Dwarf (+5)" is a `check-modifier` gated on `{ "bearer.itemNames": { "$includes": "King under the Mountain" } }`, and "other Dwarves (+2)" is the same `bearer.race: "dwarf"` shape every other Dwarf faction (Blue Mountain Dwarves tw-200, Iron Hill Dwarves tw-261) uses, `$and`-ed with `$not` of the King clause so the two rows stay mutually exclusive on the card itself (td-126's own separate, general "+5 vs Dwarves and Dwarf factions" ability still stacks on top, since it is a different card's independent effect).

Also reuses the pre-existing `playable-at-tapped-site` play-flag (Snaga-hai le-286) for "a tapped **or untapped** site" — most factions require an untapped site.

Used by *Returned Exiles* (td-146).
